	public class FireScenario
	{
		internal static CalculationStatusEnum CalculateDimensionlessSuperficialVelocity(ref double superficialVaporVelocity_m_s, ref double bubbleRiseVelocity_m_s, ref double dimensionlessSuperficialVelocity, double vaporRate_kg_h, double vaporDensity_kg_m3, double liquidDensity_kg_mg3, double liquidSurfaceTension_dyn_cm, double vesselCrossArea_m2, DisengagementRegime regime, ref string errMsg)
		{
			double num = double.NaN;
			num = regime switch
			{
				DisengagementRegime.ChurnTurbulent => 1.53, 
				DisengagementRegime.Bubbly => 1.18, 
				_ => throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("regime"), 
			};
			superficialVaporVelocity_m_s = vaporRate_kg_h / 3600.0 / vaporDensity_kg_m3 / vesselCrossArea_m2;
			bubbleRiseVelocity_m_s = U_inf(vaporDensity_kg_m3, liquidDensity_kg_mg3, liquidSurfaceTension_dyn_cm, num);
			dimensionlessSuperficialVelocity = superficialVaporVelocity_m_s / bubbleRiseVelocity_m_s;
			return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationOK;
		}

		private static double U_inf(double vaporDensity_kg_m3, double liquidDensity_kg_mg3, double liquidSurfaceTension_dyn_cm, double regimeConstant)
		{
			double num = liquidSurfaceTension_dyn_cm * 0.001;
			return regimeConstant * Math.Pow(num * 9.80665 * (liquidDensity_kg_mg3 - vaporDensity_kg_m3), 0.25) / Math.Sqrt(liquidDensity_kg_mg3);
		}

		internal static double _CalculateTwoPhaseVaporFraction(double surfaceVoidFraction, double vaporDensity_kg_m3, double liquidDensity_kg_m3)
		{
			return surfaceVoidFraction * vaporDensity_kg_m3 / (surfaceVoidFraction * vaporDensity_kg_m3 + (1.0 - surfaceVoidFraction) * liquidDensity_kg_m3);
		}

		public static CalculationStatusEnum CalculateDisengagement(ref double superficialVaporVelocity_m_s, ref double bubbleRiseVelocity_m_s, ref double onsetLiquidLevelForSwell, ref double onsetLiquidLevelForEntrainment, ref double onsetLiquidLevel, ref bool isTwoPhaseFlow, ref double initialTwoPhaseMassFraction, ref double finalTwoPhaseMassFraction, VesselTypeEnum vesselShape, FIREHeadTypeEnum vesselBottomHeadType, FIREHeadTypeEnum vesselTopHeadType, HeadsInFireArea vesselIncludedHeads, double vesselDiameter_m, double vesselTanTan_m, double vesselLiquidLevel_m, double vaporRate_kg_h, double vaporDensity_kg_m3, double liquidDensity_kg_m3, double liquidSurfaceTension_dyn_cm, DisengagementRegime disengagementRegime, DisengagementVesselFlowModel vesselFlowModelType, double C0, ref string errMsg)
		{
			if ((vesselShape == VesselTypeEnum.Vertical_BotHead || vesselShape == VesselTypeEnum.Vertical_NoBotHead || vesselShape == VesselTypeEnum.Vertical) && !_ValuesDiffer(vesselLiquidLevel_m, vesselTanTan_m))
			{
				vesselLiquidLevel_m += HeadLengthForLevel(vesselShape, vesselTopHeadType, vesselDiameter_m);
			}
			CalculationStatusEnum result = _CalculateDisengagementImpl(ref superficialVaporVelocity_m_s, ref bubbleRiseVelocity_m_s, ref onsetLiquidLevelForSwell, ref onsetLiquidLevelForEntrainment, ref onsetLiquidLevel, ref isTwoPhaseFlow, ref initialTwoPhaseMassFraction, ref finalTwoPhaseMassFraction, vesselShape, vesselBottomHeadType, vesselTopHeadType, vesselIncludedHeads, vesselDiameter_m, vesselTanTan_m, vesselLiquidLevel_m, vaporRate_kg_h, vaporDensity_kg_m3, liquidDensity_kg_m3, liquidSurfaceTension_dyn_cm, disengagementRegime, vesselFlowModelType, C0, ref errMsg);
			if (vesselShape == VesselTypeEnum.Vertical_BotHead || vesselShape == VesselTypeEnum.Vertical_NoBotHead || vesselShape == VesselTypeEnum.Vertical)
			{
				onsetLiquidLevelForSwell = Math.Max(0.0, onsetLiquidLevelForSwell);
				onsetLiquidLevelForEntrainment = Math.Max(0.0, onsetLiquidLevelForEntrainment);
				onsetLiquidLevel = Math.Max(0.0, onsetLiquidLevel);
				onsetLiquidLevelForSwell = Math.Min(vesselTanTan_m, onsetLiquidLevelForSwell);
				onsetLiquidLevelForEntrainment = Math.Min(vesselTanTan_m, onsetLiquidLevelForEntrainment);
				onsetLiquidLevel = Math.Min(vesselTanTan_m, onsetLiquidLevel);
			}
			return result;
		}

		private static CalculationStatusEnum _CalculateDisengagementImpl(ref double superficialVaporVelocity_m_s, ref double bubbleRiseVelocity_m_s, ref double onsetLiquidLevelForSwell, ref double onsetLiquidLevelForEntrainment, ref double onsetLiquidLevel, ref bool isTwoPhaseFlow, ref double initialTwoPhaseMassFraction, ref double finalTwoPhaseMassFraction, VesselTypeEnum vesselShape, FIREHeadTypeEnum vesselBottomHeadType, FIREHeadTypeEnum vesselTopHeadType, HeadsInFireArea vesselIncludedHeads, double vesselDiameter_m, double vesselTanTan_m, double vesselLiquidLevel_m, double vaporRate_kg_h, double vaporDensity_kg_m3, double liquidDensity_kg_m3, double liquidSurfaceTension_dyn_cm, DisengagementRegime disengagementRegime, DisengagementVesselFlowModel vesselFlowModelType, double C0, ref string errMsg)
		{
			superficialVaporVelocity_m_s = double.NaN;
			bubbleRiseVelocity_m_s = double.NaN;
			initialTwoPhaseMassFraction = double.NaN;
			onsetLiquidLevelForSwell = double.NaN;
			onsetLiquidLevelForEntrainment = double.NaN;
			onsetLiquidLevel = double.NaN;
			double num = double.NaN;
			double num2 = double.NaN;
			double num3 = double.NaN;
			DisengagementModel disengagementModel;
			VesselModel vesselModel;
			switch (disengagementRegime)
			{
			case DisengagementRegime.ChurnTurbulent:
				disengagementModel = new ChurnTurbulentModel();
				vesselModel = vesselFlowModelType switch
				{
					DisengagementVesselFlowModel.TotalBoiling => new TotalBoilingCTModel(), 
					DisengagementVesselFlowModel.WallBoiling => new WallBoilingCTModel(), 
					_ => throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("vesselFlowModelType"), 
				};
				break;
			case DisengagementRegime.Bubbly:
				disengagementModel = new BubblyModel();
				if ((uint)vesselFlowModelType <= 1u)
				{
					vesselModel = new BubblyVesselModel();
					break;
				}
				throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("vesselFlowModelType");
			default:
				throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("disengagementRegime");
			}
			VesselGeometry vesselGeometry;
			switch (vesselShape)
			{
			case VesselTypeEnum.Horizontal:
				vesselGeometry = new HorizontalVessel(vesselDiameter_m, vesselTanTan_m);
				break;
			case VesselTypeEnum.Vertical_BotHead:
			case VesselTypeEnum.Vertical_NoBotHead:
			case VesselTypeEnum.Vertical:
				vesselGeometry = new VerticalVessel(vesselDiameter_m, vesselTanTan_m);
				break;
			case VesselTypeEnum.Sphere:
				vesselGeometry = new SphericalVessel(vesselDiameter_m);
				break;
			default:
				throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("vesselShape");
			}
			double dimensionlessSuperficialVelocity = double.NaN;
			CalculationStatusEnum calculationStatusEnum = CalculateDimensionlessSuperficialVelocity(ref superficialVaporVelocity_m_s, ref bubbleRiseVelocity_m_s, ref dimensionlessSuperficialVelocity, vaporRate_kg_h, vaporDensity_kg_m3, liquidDensity_kg_m3, liquidSurfaceTension_dyn_cm, vesselGeometry.CalculateCrossSectionalArea_m2(), disengagementRegime, ref errMsg);
			if (calculationStatusEnum == CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationError)
			{
				return calculationStatusEnum;
			}
			num2 = disengagementModel.OnsetVoidFractionForEntrainment(vaporDensity_kg_m3, liquidDensity_kg_m3, vesselGeometry.Height_m, vesselGeometry.CalculateCrossSectionalArea_m2(), dimensionlessSuperficialVelocity);
			if (num2 >= 1.0)
			{
				errMsg = Resources.FireDisengagementFlowTooHighForEntrainment;
				return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationError;
			}
			num = vesselModel.OnsetVoidFractionForSwell(dimensionlessSuperficialVelocity, vesselShape, vesselIncludedHeads, C0);
			num3 = num2 + num * (1.0 - num2);
			double num4 = _CalculateVesselVoidFraction(vesselShape, vesselBottomHeadType, vesselTopHeadType, vesselDiameter_m, vesselTanTan_m, vesselLiquidLevel_m);
			isTwoPhaseFlow = num4 < num3;
			if (!double.IsNaN(num))
			{
				onsetLiquidLevelForSwell = _CalculateLevelFromVoidFraction(vesselShape, vesselBottomHeadType, vesselTopHeadType, vesselDiameter_m, vesselTanTan_m, num);
			}
			if (!double.IsNaN(num2))
			{
				onsetLiquidLevelForEntrainment = _CalculateLevelFromVoidFraction(vesselShape, vesselBottomHeadType, vesselTopHeadType, vesselDiameter_m, vesselTanTan_m, num2);
			}
			if (!double.IsNaN(num3))
			{
				onsetLiquidLevel = _CalculateLevelFromVoidFraction(vesselShape, vesselBottomHeadType, vesselTopHeadType, vesselDiameter_m, vesselTanTan_m, num3);
			}
			if (!isTwoPhaseFlow)
			{
				return calculationStatusEnum;
			}
			double num5 = disengagementModel.CalculateSurfaceVaporMassFraction(num4, vaporDensity_kg_m3, liquidDensity_kg_m3, C0);
			double num6 = disengagementModel.CalculateSurfaceVaporMassFraction(num, vaporDensity_kg_m3, liquidDensity_kg_m3, C0);
			if (num5 < 0.0)
			{
				errMsg = Resources.FireDisengagementVaporFraction;
				calculationStatusEnum = CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationWarning;
			}
			else if (num5 > 1.0)
			{
				errMsg = Resources.FireDisengagementVaporFractionAllVapor;
				calculationStatusEnum = CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationWarning;
				initialTwoPhaseMassFraction = 1.0;
				finalTwoPhaseMassFraction = 1.0;
			}
			else
			{
				initialTwoPhaseMassFraction = num5;
				finalTwoPhaseMassFraction = num6;
			}
			return calculationStatusEnum;
		}

		private static double _CalculateVesselVoidFraction(VesselTypeEnum vesselShape, FIREHeadTypeEnum vesselBottomHeadType, FIREHeadTypeEnum vesselTopHeadType, double vesselDiameter_m, double vesselTanTan_m, double vesselLiquidLevel_m)
		{
			double liqLevel = ((vesselShape == VesselTypeEnum.Vertical_BotHead || vesselShape == VesselTypeEnum.Vertical_NoBotHead || vesselShape == VesselTypeEnum.Vertical) ? vesselTanTan_m : vesselDiameter_m) + HeadLengthForLevel(vesselShape, vesselTopHeadType, vesselDiameter_m);
			double num = PartialVolume(vesselShape, vesselBottomHeadType, vesselTopHeadType, vesselTanTan_m, vesselDiameter_m, liqLevel, VerticalLevelBasis.IncludeHeads);
			double num2 = PartialVolume(vesselShape, vesselBottomHeadType, vesselTopHeadType, vesselTanTan_m, vesselDiameter_m, vesselLiquidLevel_m, VerticalLevelBasis.IncludeHeads);
			return 1.0 - num2 / num;
		}

		private static double _CalculateLevelFromVoidFraction(VesselTypeEnum vesselShape, FIREHeadTypeEnum vesselBottomHeadType, FIREHeadTypeEnum vesselTopHeadType, double vesselDiameter_m, double vesselTanTan_m, double voidFraction)
		{
			voidFraction = Math.Max(0.0, voidFraction);
			voidFraction = Math.Min(1.0, voidFraction);
			double num = ((vesselShape == VesselTypeEnum.Vertical_BotHead || vesselShape == VesselTypeEnum.Vertical_NoBotHead || vesselShape == VesselTypeEnum.Vertical) ? vesselTanTan_m : vesselDiameter_m) + HeadLengthForLevel(vesselShape, vesselTopHeadType, vesselDiameter_m);
			double xmin = 0.0 - HeadLengthForLevel(vesselShape, vesselBottomHeadType, vesselDiameter_m);
			double num2 = PartialVolume(vesselShape, vesselBottomHeadType, vesselTopHeadType, vesselTanTan_m, vesselDiameter_m, num, VerticalLevelBasis.IncludeHeads);
			double num3 = (1.0 - voidFraction) * num2;
			return RootFinder.RootFinder([SpecialName] (double ll) => num3 - PartialVolume(vesselShape, vesselBottomHeadType, vesselTopHeadType, vesselTanTan_m, vesselDiameter_m, ll, VerticalLevelBasis.IncludeHeads), 0.5 * num, xmin, num);
		}

		public static CalculationStatusEnum CalculateDisengagementTwoPhaseRequirement(ref double requiredArea_in2, ref double requiredRelief_kgh, ref double requiredReliefVaporFraction, ref double relievingTemp_C, FireAreaStandard calculationStandard, IPPPFluid fluid, double setPressure_kPa, double fullOpenPressure_kPa, double finalReliefPressure_kPa, VesselTypeEnum vesselShape, FIREHeadTypeEnum vesselBottomHeadType, FIREHeadTypeEnum vesselTopHeadType, HeadsInFireArea vesselIncludedHeads, double vesselDiameter_m, double vesselTanTan_m, double vesselElevation_m, double vesselLiquidLevel_m, double maxFlameHeight_m, double additionalPipingArea_pct, double additionalArea_m2, bool isAdequateDrainage, double wettedAreaExponent, bool isCalculateLatentHeat, double latentHeat_kJkg, bool isCalculatedF, double F, double insulationConductivity_USC, double insulationThickness_in, DisengagementRegime disengagementRegime, DisengagementVesselFlowModel vesselFlowModelType, double C0, ValveTypeEnum valveType, bool isCalculatedKb, double Kb, double Kc, double Kd, double Kv, double totalBackpressure_psia, double atmPressure_kPa, double inletLoss_psi, ref string errMsg)
		{
			bool canFlashByTemperature = true;
			bool flag = false;
			requiredArea_in2 = double.NaN;
			requiredRelief_kgh = double.NaN;
			double num = double.NaN;
			if (vesselShape == VesselTypeEnum.Vertical_BotHead || vesselShape == VesselTypeEnum.Vertical_NoBotHead || vesselShape == VesselTypeEnum.Vertical)
			{
				num = vesselTanTan_m;
				if (!_ValuesDiffer(vesselLiquidLevel_m, num))
				{
					vesselLiquidLevel_m += HeadLengthForLevel(vesselShape, vesselTopHeadType, vesselDiameter_m);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				num = vesselDiameter_m;
			}
			double num2 = TotalVolume(vesselShape, vesselDiameter_m, vesselTanTan_m, vesselBottomHeadType, vesselTopHeadType);
			double num3 = PartialVolume(vesselShape, vesselBottomHeadType, vesselTopHeadType, vesselTanTan_m, vesselDiameter_m, vesselLiquidLevel_m, VerticalLevelBasis.IncludeHeads);
			double num4 = num2 - num3;
			double num5 = num3 * fluid.LiquidMassDensity();
			double num6 = num4 * fluid.VaporMassDensity();
			if (double.IsNaN(num6))
			{
				num6 = 0.0;
			}
			double num7 = num5 + num6;
			double num8 = num5 / fluid.LiquidMolecularWeight();
			double num9 = num6 / fluid.VaporMolecularWeight();
			if (double.IsNaN(num9))
			{
				num9 = 0.0;
			}
			double density_kgm = num7 / num2;
			double liquidVaporRatio = Math.Min(num9 / (num8 + num9), 1.0);
			if (num4 > 1E-08 && (!(fluid.VaporFraction() > 0.0) || !(fluid.VaporFraction() < 1.0)))
			{
				errMsg = Resources.FireDisengagementAllLiquid;
				return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationError;
			}
			fluid.SetLiquidVaporRatio(liquidVaporRatio);
			if (num6 > 0.0)
			{
				canFlashByTemperature = !FluidExtensions.IsSingleComponentStream(fluid);
				flag = true;
			}
			if (!FluidExtensions.ConstantDensityFlash(fluid, fullOpenPressure_kPa, density_kgm, ref canFlashByTemperature))
			{
				errMsg = Resources.FireDisengagementIsochoricFailed;
				return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationError;
			}
			double num10 = (1.0 - fluid.VaporFraction()) * (num8 + num9) * fluid.LiquidMolecularWeight() / fluid.LiquidMassDensity();
			double voidFraction = 1.0 - num10 / num2;
			_CalculateLevelFromVoidFraction(vesselShape, vesselBottomHeadType, vesselTopHeadType, vesselDiameter_m, vesselTanTan_m, voidFraction);
			double num11 = num10 * fluid.LiquidMassDensity();
			if (!fluid.PVFlashLiquid(fullOpenPressure_kPa, 0.0001))
			{
				errMsg = Resources.FireDisengagementBubblePointFailed;
				return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationError;
			}
			if (!FluidExtensions.ConstantDensityFlash(fluid, fullOpenPressure_kPa, num11 / num2, ref canFlashByTemperature))
			{
				errMsg = Resources.FireDisengagementIsochoricFailed;
				return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationError;
			}
			double num12 = (1.0 - FluidExtensions.VaporVolumeFraction(fluid)) * num2;
			double num13 = FluidExtensions.VaporVolumeFraction(fluid);
			if (fluid.VaporFraction() == 0.0)
			{
				if (!fluid.PVFlash(fullOpenPressure_kPa, 0.0001))
				{
					errMsg = Resources.FireDisengagementBubblePointFailed;
					return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationError;
				}
				num11 = num2 * fluid.LiquidMassDensity();
			}
			double vesselLiquidLevel_m2 = _CalculateLevelFromVoidFraction(vesselShape, vesselBottomHeadType, vesselTopHeadType, vesselDiameter_m, vesselTanTan_m, num13);
			double num14 = num12 * fluid.LiquidMassDensity() / fluid.LiquidMolecularWeight();
			double num15 = (num2 - num12) * fluid.VaporMassDensity() / fluid.VaporMolecularWeight();
			if (double.IsNaN(num15))
			{
				num15 = 0.0;
			}
			double num16 = fluid.LiquidMolecularWeight();
			double num17 = fluid.VaporMolecularWeight();
			fluid.StoreOverallFractions();
			double num18 = FluidExtensions.OverallMolarEnthalpy(fluid);
			double num19 = fluid.VaporMassDensity();
			if (isCalculateLatentHeat)
			{
				if (!fluid.PVFlashLiquid(fullOpenPressure_kPa, 0.0001))
				{
					errMsg = Resources.FireDisengagementBubblePointFailed;
					return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationError;
				}
				if (double.IsNaN(num19))
				{
					num19 = fluid.VaporMassDensity();
					num17 = fluid.VaporMolecularWeight();
				}
				latentHeat_kJkg = FluidExtensions.CalculateLatentHeat(fluid, 0.0, 0.0002);
				fluid.RevertOverallFractions();
				fluid.PHFlash(fullOpenPressure_kPa, num18);
			}
			else if (double.IsNaN(num19))
			{
				if (!fluid.PVFlashLiquid(fullOpenPressure_kPa, 0.0001))
				{
					errMsg = Resources.FireDisengagementBubblePointFailed;
					return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationError;
				}
				num19 = fluid.VaporMassDensity();
				num17 = fluid.VaporMolecularWeight();
				fluid.RevertOverallFractions();
				fluid.PHFlash(fullOpenPressure_kPa, num18);
			}
			double FIRECalcArea = double.NaN;
			if (Calculate_Fire_Area(ref FIRECalcArea, vesselShape, isWetted: true, additionalPipingArea_pct > 0.0, 1, maxFlameHeight_m, vesselTanTan_m, vesselElevation_m, num, vesselDiameter_m, additionalPipingArea_pct, additionalArea_m2, vesselBottomHeadType, vesselTopHeadType, vesselIncludedHeads, calculationStandard, ref errMsg) == CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationError)
			{
				return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationError;
			}
			double HeatInput = double.NaN;
			if (FireHeatInput(ref HeatInput, calculationStandard, isAdequateDrainage, isCalculatedF, ref F, insulationConductivity_USC, insulationThickness_in, fluid.Temperature(), FIRECalcArea, 1, wettedAreaExponent, ref errMsg) == CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationError)
			{
				return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationError;
			}
			double num20 = HeatInput * 1.055056;
			double vaporRate_kg_h = num20 / latentHeat_kJkg;
			double superficialVaporVelocity_m_s = double.NaN;
			double bubbleRiseVelocity_m_s = double.NaN;
			double onsetLiquidLevelForSwell = double.NaN;
			double onsetLiquidLevelForEntrainment = double.NaN;
			double onsetLiquidLevel = double.NaN;
			double initialTwoPhaseMassFraction = double.NaN;
			double finalTwoPhaseMassFraction = double.NaN;
			bool isTwoPhaseFlow = default(bool);
			_CalculateDisengagementImpl(ref superficialVaporVelocity_m_s, ref bubbleRiseVelocity_m_s, ref onsetLiquidLevelForSwell, ref onsetLiquidLevelForEntrainment, ref onsetLiquidLevel, ref isTwoPhaseFlow, ref initialTwoPhaseMassFraction, ref finalTwoPhaseMassFraction, vesselShape, vesselBottomHeadType, vesselTopHeadType, vesselIncludedHeads, vesselDiameter_m, vesselTanTan_m, vesselLiquidLevel_m2, vaporRate_kg_h, num19, fluid.LiquidMassDensity(), fluid.LiquidSurfaceTension(), disengagementRegime, vesselFlowModelType, C0, ref errMsg);
			double num21 = PartialVolume(vesselShape, vesselBottomHeadType, vesselTopHeadType, vesselTanTan_m, vesselDiameter_m, onsetLiquidLevelForSwell, VerticalLevelBasis.IncludeHeads);
			double num22 = PartialVolume(vesselShape, vesselBottomHeadType, vesselTopHeadType, vesselTanTan_m, vesselDiameter_m, onsetLiquidLevelForEntrainment, VerticalLevelBasis.IncludeHeads);
			double num23 = PartialVolume(vesselShape, vesselBottomHeadType, vesselTopHeadType, vesselTanTan_m, vesselDiameter_m, onsetLiquidLevel, VerticalLevelBasis.IncludeHeads);
			_ = num21 / num2;
			_ = num22 / num2;
			if (1.0 - num23 / num2 <= num13)
			{
				requiredArea_in2 = 0.0;
				requiredRelief_kgh = 0.0;
				errMsg = Resources.FireDisengagementDisengagedAtFullFlow;
				return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationWarning;
			}
			double num24 = (num12 - num23) * fluid.LiquidMassDensity();
			double num25 = num24 / fluid.LiquidMolecularWeight();
			double num26 = num24 * fluid.LiquidMassEnthalpy();
			double num27 = Math.Max(initialTwoPhaseMassFraction, finalTwoPhaseMassFraction);
			double num28 = num27 / num17 / (num27 / num17 + (1.0 - num27) / num16);
			double num29 = num11 * fluid.OverallMassEnthalpy() - num26;
			if (!fluid.PVFlashLiquid(fullOpenPressure_kPa, num28))
			{
				errMsg = Resources.FireDisengagementVaporFractionFlashFailed;
				return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationError;
			}
			relievingTemp_C = fluid.Temperature();
			fluid.StoreFireFractions(FluidPhaseSelection.Overall);
			fluid.RevertOverallFractions();
			fluid.PHFlash(fullOpenPressure_kPa, num18);
			double liquidVaporRatio2 = num15 / (num15 + num14 - num25);
			fluid.SetLiquidVaporRatio(liquidVaporRatio2);
			if (canFlashByTemperature && !flag)
			{
				canFlashByTemperature = !FluidExtensions.IsSingleComponentStream(fluid);
			}
			double density_kgm2 = (num11 - num24) / num2;
			if (!FluidExtensions.ConstantDensityFlash(fluid, finalReliefPressure_kPa, density_kgm2, ref canFlashByTemperature, num18))
			{
				errMsg = Resources.FireDisengagementIsochoricFailed;
				return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationError;
			}
			double num30 = ((num11 - num24) * fluid.OverallMassEnthalpy() - num29) / num20;
			requiredRelief_kgh = num24 / num30;
			requiredReliefVaporFraction = num28;
			bool flowType_isCritical = false;
			double MassFlux_Design = double.NaN;
			double overPress = Math.Round(100.0 * ((fullOpenPressure_kPa - atmPressure_kPa) / (setPressure_kPa - atmPressure_kPa) - 1.0), 6);
			if (Mixed_Relief.CalculateOrificeSize_4(ref requiredArea_in2, ref flowType_isCritical, ref MassFlux_Design, new List<Mixed_Relief.DirectIntegrationStep>(), requiredRelief_kgh * 2.20462, double.NaN, num28, valveType, ref Kb, Kc, Kd, Kv, isCalculatedKb, setPressure_kPa * 0.1450377, overPress, totalBackpressure_psia, fullOpenPressure_kPa * 0.1450377, inletLoss_psi, fluid, IsFireCalc: true, shouldReflash: true, ref errMsg) == CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationError)
			{
				return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationError;
			}
			errMsg = string.Empty;
			return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationOK;
		}

		private static bool _ValuesDiffer(double num1, double num2, double relativeErrorThreshold = 1E-10, double zeroThreshold = 1E-10)
		{
			return MyMath.ValuesDiffer(num1, num2, relativeErrorThreshold, zeroThreshold);
		}

		public static CalculationStatusEnum VesselCalculations(ref double FIREHeatInput, ref double FIRERelFlow, ref double FIRECalcArea, VesselTypeEnum VesselType, bool isWetted, bool isDrainage, bool isAreaCalculated, bool pipingContributes, short VesselNumber, double FIREFlameHeight, double FIREHeight, double FIREVesselElevation, double FIRELiqLevel, double FIREDiameter, double FIREPipingSurfaceRatio, double FIREAdditionalArea, double FIREFluidLatHeat, double FIRECoeff_F, FIREHeadTypeEnum FIREHeadBottomType, FIREHeadTypeEnum FIREHeadTopType, HeadsInFireArea FIREIncludedHeads, double FIREWettedAreaExponent, FireAreaStandard FIREStandard, ref string returnMsg)
		{
			CalculationStatusEnum calculationStatusEnum = CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationOK;
			if (isAreaCalculated)
			{
				calculationStatusEnum = Calculate_Fire_Area(ref FIRECalcArea, VesselType, isWetted, pipingContributes, VesselNumber, FIREFlameHeight, FIREHeight, FIREVesselElevation, FIRELiqLevel, FIREDiameter, FIREPipingSurfaceRatio, FIREAdditionalArea, FIREHeadBottomType, FIREHeadTopType, FIREIncludedHeads, FIREStandard, ref returnMsg);
				if (calculationStatusEnum == CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationError)
				{
					return calculationStatusEnum;
				}
			}
			double num = FIRECalcArea * 10.76391041670972;
			if (isWetted)
			{
				if (FIREStandard == FireAreaStandard.Api521 || MyMath.ValueExceeds(num, 2800.0, 1E-06, 1E-10))
				{
					if (FIREStandard != FireAreaStandard.Api521)
					{
						FIREWettedAreaExponent = 0.82;
					}
					if (isDrainage || FIREStandard != FireAreaStandard.Api521)
					{
						FIREHeatInput = 21000.0 * Math.Pow(num, FIREWettedAreaExponent);
					}
					else
					{
						FIREHeatInput = 34500.0 * Math.Pow(num, FIREWettedAreaExponent);
					}
					if (FIREStandard == FireAreaStandard.Api521)
					{
						FIREHeatInput *= FIRECoeff_F;
					}
				}
				else if (!MyMath.ValueExceeds(num, 200.0, 1E-06, 1E-10))
				{
					FIREHeatInput = 20000.0 * num;
				}
				else if (!MyMath.ValueExceeds(num, 1000.0, 1E-06, 1E-10))
				{
					FIREHeatInput = 199300.0 * Math.Pow(num, 0.566);
				}
				else if (!MyMath.ValueExceeds(num, 2800.0, 1E-06, 1E-10))
				{
					FIREHeatInput = 963400.0 * Math.Pow(num, 0.338);
				}
				FIREHeatInput *= 1.055056;
				if (FIRECalcArea == 0.0)
				{
					if (FIREFluidLatHeat == 0.0)
					{
						FIREFluidLatHeat = 25.0;
					}
				}
				else if (FIREFluidLatHeat == 0.0)
				{
					returnMsg = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("EnterLatHeat") + Conversions.ToString((int)VesselNumber) + ")!";
					return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationError;
				}
				FIRERelFlow = FIREHeatInput / FIREFluidLatHeat;
				if (FIREStandard != FireAreaStandard.Api521)
				{
					FIRERelFlow *= FIRECoeff_F;
				}
			}
			return calculationStatusEnum;
		}

		public static CalculationStatusEnum Calculate_F(ref double Coeff_F, double FIREInsThermCond, double FIREFluidTemp, double FIREInsThk, int VesselNumber, ref string returnMsg)
		{
			Coeff_F = FIREInsThermCond * (1660.0 - FIREFluidTemp) / 21000.0 / FIREInsThk;
			if ((Coeff_F > 1.0) | (Coeff_F <= 0.0))
			{
				returnMsg = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("FOutside") + Conversions.ToString(VesselNumber) + ").";
				Coeff_F = 1.0;
				return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationError;
			}
			return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationOK;
		}

		public static CalculationStatusEnum Calculate_Fire_Area(ref double FIRECalcArea, VesselTypeEnum VesselType, bool isWetted, bool pipingContributes, short VesselNumber, double FIREFlameHeight, double FIREHeight, double FIREVesselElevation, double FIRELiqLevel, double FIREDiameter, double FIREPipingSurfaceRatio, double FIREAdditionalArea, FIREHeadTypeEnum FIREBottomHeadType, FIREHeadTypeEnum FIRETopHeadType, HeadsInFireArea FIREIncludedHeads, FireAreaStandard FIREStandard, ref string returnMsg)
		{
			CalculationStatusEnum result = CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationOK;
			double num = 0.0;
			double num2 = 3.14159265;
			FIRECalcArea = 0.0;
			if (FIREStandard == FireAreaStandard.NFPA30 || !isWetted)
			{
				FIRELiqLevel = ((!(VesselType == VesselTypeEnum.Sphere || VesselType == VesselTypeEnum.Horizontal)) ? FIREHeight : FIREDiameter);
			}
			FIRELiqLevel = ((!(VesselType == VesselTypeEnum.Sphere || VesselType == VesselTypeEnum.Horizontal)) ? Math.Min(FIRELiqLevel, FIREHeight) : Math.Min(FIRELiqLevel, FIREDiameter));
			num = ((VesselType == VesselTypeEnum.Sphere && FIREStandard == FireAreaStandard.Api521) ? Math.Min(FIRELiqLevel, Math.Max(FIREFlameHeight - FIREVesselElevation, FIREDiameter / 2.0)) : ((!(FIRELiqLevel + FIREVesselElevation > FIREFlameHeight)) ? FIRELiqLevel : (FIREFlameHeight - FIREVesselElevation)));
			double num7 = default(double);
			switch (VesselType)
			{
			case VesselTypeEnum.Horizontal:
				if (num > 0.0)
				{
					double num3 = Math.Min((num - FIREDiameter / 2.0) / (FIREDiameter / 2.0), 1.0);
					double num5;
					if (num3 == 1.0)
					{
						num5 = num2 * FIREDiameter * FIREHeight;
					}
					else
					{
						double num6 = Math.Atan(num3 / Math.Pow((0.0 - num3) * num3 + 1.0, 0.5));
						num5 = num2 * FIREDiameter / 2.0 * FIREHeight + num6 * FIREDiameter * FIREHeight;
					}
					if (FIREIncludedHeads == HeadsInFireArea.Both || FIREIncludedHeads == HeadsInFireArea.BottomOnly)
					{
						num7 += _CalculateHorizontalHeadWettedArea(FIREBottomHeadType, FIREDiameter, num);
					}
					if (FIREIncludedHeads == HeadsInFireArea.Both || FIREIncludedHeads == HeadsInFireArea.TopOnly)
					{
						num7 += _CalculateHorizontalHeadWettedArea(FIRETopHeadType, FIREDiameter, num);
					}
					FIRECalcArea = num5 + num7;
				}
				else
				{
					FIRECalcArea = 0.0;
					returnMsg = Resources.FlameLow + Conversions.ToString((int)VesselNumber) + ".";
					result = CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationWarning;
				}
				break;
			case VesselTypeEnum.Vertical_BotHead:
			case VesselTypeEnum.Vertical:
				if (num < 0.0)
				{
					returnMsg = Resources.FlameLow + Conversions.ToString((int)VesselNumber) + ".";
					result = CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationWarning;
				}
				num = Math.Max(0.0, num);
				if (FIREIncludedHeads == HeadsInFireArea.Both || FIREIncludedHeads == HeadsInFireArea.BottomOnly)
				{
					num7 += _CalculateVerticalHeadWettedArea(FIREBottomHeadType, FIREDiameter);
				}
				if ((FIREIncludedHeads == HeadsInFireArea.Both || FIREIncludedHeads == HeadsInFireArea.TopOnly) && (!_ValuesDiffer(num, FIREHeight) || num > FIREHeight))
				{
					num7 += _CalculateVerticalHeadWettedArea(FIRETopHeadType, FIREDiameter);
				}
				FIRECalcArea = Math.PI * FIREDiameter * num + num7;
				break;
			case VesselTypeEnum.Vertical_NoBotHead:
				if (num > 0.0)
				{
					FIRECalcArea = num2 * FIREDiameter * num;
					break;
				}
				FIRECalcArea = 0.0;
				returnMsg = Resources.FlameLow + Conversions.ToString((int)VesselNumber) + ".";
				result = CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationWarning;
				break;
			case VesselTypeEnum.Sphere:
			{
				double num3 = Math.Max((FIREDiameter / 2.0 - num) / (FIREDiameter / 2.0), -1.0);
				if (num3 == -1.0)
				{
					FIRECalcArea = 4.0 * num2 * Math.Pow(FIREDiameter / 2.0, 2.0);
					break;
				}
				if (num3 == 1.0)
				{
					FIRECalcArea = 0.0;
					break;
				}
				double num4 = Math.Atan((0.0 - num3) / Math.Pow((0.0 - num3) * num3 + 1.0, 0.5)) + 2.0 * Math.Atan(1.0);
				FIRECalcArea = 2.0 * num2 * Math.Pow(FIREDiameter / 2.0, 2.0) * (Math.Sin(num4 - num2 / 2.0) + 1.0);
				break;
			}
			}
			if (FIREStandard == FireAreaStandard.Api2000 || FIREStandard == FireAreaStandard.NFPA30)
			{
				switch (VesselType)
				{
				case VesselTypeEnum.Horizontal:
				{
					double num9 = Math.PI * FIREDiameter * FIREHeight;
					double num10 = 0.0;
					if (FIREIncludedHeads == HeadsInFireArea.Both || FIREIncludedHeads == HeadsInFireArea.BottomOnly)
					{
						num10 += _CalculateHorizontalHeadWettedArea(FIREBottomHeadType, FIREDiameter, FIREDiameter);
					}
					if (FIREIncludedHeads == HeadsInFireArea.Both || FIREIncludedHeads == HeadsInFireArea.TopOnly)
					{
						num10 += _CalculateHorizontalHeadWettedArea(FIRETopHeadType, FIREDiameter, FIREDiameter);
					}
					double num11 = 0.75 * (num9 + num10);
					if (FIREStandard == FireAreaStandard.Api2000)
					{
						FIRECalcArea = Math.Max(FIRECalcArea, num11);
					}
					else
					{
						FIRECalcArea = num11;
					}
					break;
				}
				case VesselTypeEnum.Sphere:
				{
					double num8 = 0.55 * (Math.PI * 4.0 * Math.Pow(FIREDiameter / 2.0, 2.0));
					if (FIREStandard == FireAreaStandard.Api2000)
					{
						FIRECalcArea = Math.Max(FIRECalcArea, num8);
					}
					else
					{
						FIRECalcArea = num8;
					}
					break;
				}
				}
			}
			if (pipingContributes)
			{
				FIRECalcArea *= 1.0 + FIREPipingSurfaceRatio / 100.0;
			}
			if (!double.IsNaN(FIREAdditionalArea))
			{
				FIRECalcArea += FIREAdditionalArea;
			}
			return result;
		}

		private static double _CalculateHorizontalHeadWettedArea(FIREHeadTypeEnum headType, double diameter, double level)
		{
			switch (headType)
			{
			case FIREHeadTypeEnum.Ellipsoidal:
			{
				double num2 = level / diameter;
				return Math.PI / 8.0 * diameter * diameter * (Math.Sqrt(1.0 + 12.0 * Math.Pow(num2 - 0.5, 2.0)) * (num2 - 0.5) + 1.0 + 0.2887 * Math.Log((3.464 * (num2 - 0.5) + Math.Sqrt(1.0 + 12.0 * Math.Pow(num2 - 0.5, 2.0))) / (2.0 - Math.Sqrt(3.0))));
			}
			case FIREHeadTypeEnum.Flat:
			{
				double num = 2.0 * Math.Acos((diameter - 2.0 * level) / diameter);
				return 0.125 * Math.Pow(diameter, 2.0) * (num - Math.Sin(num));
			}
			case FIREHeadTypeEnum.Hemispherical:
				return Math.PI * level * diameter / 2.0;
			default:
				throw new InvalidOperationException($"Internal error: Unexpected head type {headType}");
			}
		}

		private static double _CalculateVerticalHeadWettedArea(FIREHeadTypeEnum headType, double diameter)
		{
			return headType switch
			{
				FIREHeadTypeEnum.Ellipsoidal => 1.084 * Math.Pow(diameter, 2.0), 
				FIREHeadTypeEnum.Hemispherical => Math.PI / 2.0 * Math.Pow(diameter, 2.0), 
				FIREHeadTypeEnum.Flat => Math.PI / 4.0 * Math.Pow(diameter, 2.0), 
				_ => throw new InvalidOperationException($"Internal error: Unexpected head type {headType}"), 
			};
		}

		private static double _CalculateFullHorizontalHeadArea(FIREHeadTypeEnum headType, double diameter)
		{
			switch (headType)
			{
			case FIREHeadTypeEnum.Flat:
				return Math.PI * Math.Pow(diameter / 2.0, 2.0);
			case FIREHeadTypeEnum.Hemispherical:
				return Math.PI * 2.0 * Math.Pow(diameter / 2.0, 2.0);
			case FIREHeadTypeEnum.Ellipsoidal:
			{
				double num = 1.0;
				return Math.PI / 8.0 * diameter * diameter * (Math.Sqrt(1.0 + 12.0 * Math.Pow(num - 0.5, 2.0)) * (num - 0.5) + 1.0 + 0.2887 * Math.Log((3.464 * (num - 0.5) + Math.Sqrt(1.0 + 12.0 * Math.Pow(num - 0.5, 2.0))) / (2.0 - Math.Sqrt(3.0))));
			}
			default:
				throw new InvalidOperationException($"Internal error: Unexpected head type {headType}");
			}
		}

		public static CalculationStatusEnum FireHeatInput(ref double HeatInput, FireAreaStandard FireStandard, bool Drainage, bool CalcF, ref double Coeff_F, double InsCond, double InsThk, double FlTemp, double Area, int VesselNumber, double WettedAreaExponent, ref string returnMsg)
		{
			CalculationStatusEnum calculationStatusEnum = CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationOK;
			if (CalcF)
			{
				calculationStatusEnum = Calculate_F(ref Coeff_F, InsCond, FlTemp, InsThk, VesselNumber, ref returnMsg);
			}
			if (calculationStatusEnum == CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationError)
			{
				return calculationStatusEnum;
			}
			double num = Area * 10.76391041670972;
			if (FireStandard == FireAreaStandard.Api521 || MyMath.ValueExceeds(num, 2800.0, 1E-06, 1E-10))
			{
				if (FireStandard != FireAreaStandard.Api521)
				{
					WettedAreaExponent = 0.82;
				}
				if (Drainage || FireStandard != FireAreaStandard.Api521)
				{
					HeatInput = 21000.0 * Math.Pow(num, WettedAreaExponent);
				}
				else
				{
					HeatInput = 34500.0 * Math.Pow(num, WettedAreaExponent);
				}
			}
			else if (!MyMath.ValueExceeds(num, 200.0, 1E-06, 1E-10))
			{
				HeatInput = 20000.0 * num;
			}
			else if (!MyMath.ValueExceeds(num, 1000.0, 1E-06, 1E-10))
			{
				HeatInput = 199300.0 * Math.Pow(num, 0.566);
			}
			else if (!MyMath.ValueExceeds(num, 2800.0, 1E-06, 1E-10))
			{
				HeatInput = 936400.0 * Math.Pow(num, 0.338);
			}
			HeatInput *= Coeff_F;
			return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationOK;
		}

		public static (CalculationStatusEnum status, string message) CalculateAreaFromVolume(ref double calcArea, ref double calcLevel, double fluidVolume, VesselTypeEnum vesselType, FIREHeadTypeEnum headBottomType, FIREHeadTypeEnum headTopType, HeadsInFireArea headsIncluded, short vesselNumber, double flameHeight, double height, double diameter, double elevation, double currentLevel, bool pipingContributes, double pipingRatio, double additionalArea, FireAreaStandard calculationStandard)
		{
			string returnMsg = null;
			CalculationStatusEnum calculationStatusEnum = CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationOK;
			double num = 0.0;
			VesselTypeEnum vesselTypeEnum = vesselType;
			double num2;
			if (vesselTypeEnum == VesselTypeEnum.Horizontal || vesselTypeEnum == VesselTypeEnum.Sphere)
			{
				num2 = diameter;
			}
			else
			{
				num2 = height;
				num -= HeadLengthForLevel(vesselType, headBottomType, diameter);
				num2 += HeadLengthForLevel(vesselType, headTopType, diameter);
			}
			(CalculationStatusEnum, string) result;
			try
			{
				calcLevel = RootFinder.RootFinder([SpecialName] (double level) => PartialVolume(vesselType, headBottomType, headTopType, height, diameter, level, VerticalLevelBasis.IncludeHeads) - fluidVolume, currentLevel, num, num2);
			}
			catch (RootFinder.RootFailedException ex)
			{
				ProjectData.SetProjectError(ex);
				RootFinder.RootFailedException ex2 = ex;
				switch (ex2.Reason)
				{
				case RootFinder.RootFailureType.OutOfBoundsHigh:
					calcLevel = num2;
					break;
				case RootFinder.RootFailureType.OutOfBoundsLow:
					calcLevel = num;
					break;
				default:
					calcArea = double.NaN;
					calcLevel = double.NaN;
					calculationStatusEnum = CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationError;
					returnMsg = string.Format(Resources.LevelFailed, vesselNumber);
					result = (calculationStatusEnum, returnMsg);
					ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
					goto IL_0186;
				}
				ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
			}
			calculationStatusEnum = Calculate_Fire_Area(ref calcArea, vesselType, isWetted: true, pipingContributes, vesselNumber, flameHeight, height, elevation, calcLevel, diameter, pipingRatio, additionalArea, headBottomType, headTopType, headsIncluded, calculationStandard, ref returnMsg);
			result = (calculationStatusEnum, returnMsg);
			goto IL_0186;
			IL_0186:
			return result;
		}

		public static double PartialVolume(VesselTypeEnum vesselType, FIREHeadTypeEnum headBottomType, FIREHeadTypeEnum headTopType, double height, double diameter, double liqLevel, VerticalLevelBasis verticalLevelBasis = VerticalLevelBasis.TangentOnly)
		{
			double result;
			switch (vesselType)
			{
			case VesselTypeEnum.Sphere:
				if (liqLevel > diameter)
				{
					liqLevel = diameter;
				}
				result = CalculatePartialVolume_Spherical(diameter, liqLevel);
				break;
			case VesselTypeEnum.Horizontal:
				if (liqLevel > diameter)
				{
					liqLevel = diameter;
				}
				result = CalculatePartialVolume_Horizontal(diameter, height, liqLevel, headBottomType, headTopType);
				break;
			default:
			{
				if (verticalLevelBasis == VerticalLevelBasis.TangentOnly && liqLevel < 1E-10)
				{
					return 0.0;
				}
				double num = height + HeadLengthForLevel(vesselType, headTopType, diameter);
				if (liqLevel > num)
				{
					liqLevel = num;
				}
				if (verticalLevelBasis == VerticalLevelBasis.TangentOnly && !_ValuesDiffer(liqLevel, height))
				{
					liqLevel = num;
				}
				result = CalculatePartialVolume_Vertical(diameter, height, liqLevel, headBottomType, headTopType);
				break;
			}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static double CalculatePartialVolume_Vertical(double Diam, double Height, double LL, FIREHeadTypeEnum headBottomType, FIREHeadTypeEnum headTopType)
		{
			double num = Math.Max(Math.Min(Height, LL), 0.0);
			double num2 = HeadLengthForLevel(VesselTypeEnum.Vertical, headBottomType, Diam);
			double val = LL + num2;
			double num3 = 0.0;
			double levelFromDish = Math.Min(val, num2);
			num3 += _CalculatePartialVolume_VerticalHead(headBottomType, Diam, levelFromDish);
			double num4 = 0.25 * Math.Pow(Diam, 2.0) * Math.PI * num;
			if (LL > num)
			{
				double num5 = LL - num;
				double num6 = HeadLengthForLevel(VesselTypeEnum.Vertical, headTopType, Diam);
				double levelFromDish2 = num6 - num5;
				double num7 = _CalculatePartialVolume_VerticalHead(headTopType, Diam, levelFromDish2);
				double num8 = _CalculatePartialVolume_VerticalHead(headTopType, Diam, num6);
				num3 += num8 - num7;
			}
			return num4 + num3;
		}

		public static double CalculatePartialVolume_Horizontal(double diam, double height, double LL, FIREHeadTypeEnum headBottomType, FIREHeadTypeEnum headTopType)
		{
			double num = diam / 2.0;
			double num2 = (num * num * Math.Acos((num - LL) / num) - (num - LL) * Math.Sqrt(2.0 * num * LL - LL * LL)) * height;
			double num3 = _CalculatePartialVolume_HorizontalHead(headBottomType, diam, LL) + _CalculatePartialVolume_HorizontalHead(headTopType, diam, LL);
			return num2 + num3;
		}

		private static double _CalculatePartialVolume_HorizontalHead(FIREHeadTypeEnum headType, double diameter, double level)
		{
			_ = diameter / 2.0;
			double num = 1.0;
			switch (headType)
			{
			case FIREHeadTypeEnum.Ellipsoidal:
			{
				double num2 = diameter / 4.0;
				double num3 = 2.0 * num2 / diameter;
				double num4;
				if (level == diameter)
				{
					num4 = Math.PI * num3 * diameter * diameter * diameter / 12.0;
				}
				else
				{
					num4 = Math.PI * num3 * diameter * diameter * diameter / 12.0;
					num = (0.0 - level / diameter) * (level / diameter) * (-3.0 + 2.0 * level / diameter);
				}
				return num4 * num;
			}
			case FIREHeadTypeEnum.Hemispherical:
				return Math.PI / 6.0 * Math.Pow(level, 2.0) * (1.5 * diameter - level);
			case FIREHeadTypeEnum.Flat:
				return 0.0;
			default:
				throw new InvalidOperationException($"Internal error: Unexpected head type {headType}");
			}
		}

		private static double _CalculatePartialVolume_VerticalHead(FIREHeadTypeEnum headType, double diameter, double levelFromDish)
		{
			switch (headType)
			{
			case FIREHeadTypeEnum.Flat:
				return 0.0;
			case FIREHeadTypeEnum.Hemispherical:
				return Math.PI * levelFromDish * levelFromDish * (1.5 * diameter - levelFromDish) / 3.0;
			case FIREHeadTypeEnum.Ellipsoidal:
			{
				double alpha = 0.875 - Math.Sqrt(0.38812499999999994);
				double num = _CalculateVerticalEllipsoidalHeadVolumeFactor(levelFromDish / diameter);
				double num2 = _CalculateVerticalEllipsoidalHeadVolumeFactor(alpha);
				return Math.PI / 24.0 * Math.Pow(diameter, 3.0) * (num / num2);
			}
			default:
				throw new InvalidOperationException($"Internal error: Unexpected head type {headType}");
			}
		}

		private static double _CalculateVerticalEllipsoidalHeadVolumeFactor(double alpha)
		{
			double num = 0.875 * (1.0 - Math.Sqrt(0.7808963331824356));
			double num2 = 0.875 - Math.Sqrt(0.38812499999999994);
			if (alpha < num)
			{
				return 0.875 * Math.Pow(alpha, 2.0) - Math.Pow(alpha, 3.0) / 3.0;
			}
			double num3 = 0.875 * Math.Pow(num, 2.0) - Math.Pow(num, 3.0) / 3.0;
			double num4 = alpha - num;
			double num5 = alpha - num2;
			double num6 = num - num2;
			double num7 = 0.028900000000000006;
			double num8 = (Math.Pow(0.32999999999999996, 2.0) + num7) * num4 - 1.0 / 3.0 * (Math.Pow(num5, 3.0) - Math.Pow(num6, 3.0)) + 0.32999999999999996 * (num5 * Math.Sqrt(num7 - Math.Pow(num5, 2.0)) - num6 * Math.Sqrt(num7 - Math.Pow(num6, 2.0)) + num7 * Math.Asin(num5 / 0.17) - num7 * Math.Asin(num6 / 0.17));
			return num3 + num8;
		}

		public static double CalculatePartialVolume_Spherical(double diameter, double liqLevel)
		{
			double num = diameter / 2.0;
			double num2 = 0.0;
			double num3 = 0.0;
			if (liqLevel <= num)
			{
				return Math.PI * liqLevel * liqLevel * (3.0 * num - liqLevel) / 3.0;
			}
			double num4 = 4.1887902047863905 * Math.Pow(num, 3.0);
			double num5 = diameter - liqLevel;
			num3 = Math.PI * num5 * num5 * (3.0 * num - num5) / 3.0;
			return num4 - num3;
		}

		public static double TotalVolume(VesselTypeEnum vesselType, double diam, double tantan, FIREHeadTypeEnum headBottomType, FIREHeadTypeEnum headTopType)
		{
			double num = 0.0;
			if (vesselType == VesselTypeEnum.Sphere)
			{
				return 4.1887902047863905 * Math.Pow(diam, 3.0) * 0.125;
			}
			double num2 = Math.PI / 4.0 * Math.Pow(diam, 2.0) * tantan;
			double num3 = 0.0;
			switch (vesselType)
			{
			case VesselTypeEnum.Horizontal:
				num3 += _CalculatePartialVolume_HorizontalHead(headBottomType, diam, diam);
				num3 += _CalculatePartialVolume_HorizontalHead(headTopType, diam, diam);
				break;
			case VesselTypeEnum.Vertical_BotHead:
			case VesselTypeEnum.Vertical_NoBotHead:
			case VesselTypeEnum.Vertical:
				num3 += _CalculatePartialVolume_VerticalHead(headBottomType, diam, HeadLengthForLevel(vesselType, headBottomType, diam));
				num3 += _CalculatePartialVolume_VerticalHead(headTopType, diam, HeadLengthForLevel(vesselType, headTopType, diam));
				break;
			default:
				throw new InvalidOperationException($"Internal error: Unexpected vessel type {vesselType}");
			case VesselTypeEnum.Sphere:
				break;
			}
			return num2 + num3;
		}

		public static double HeadLengthForLevel(VesselTypeEnum vesselType, FIREHeadTypeEnum headType, double diameter)
		{
			if (vesselType == VesselTypeEnum.Vertical_BotHead || vesselType == VesselTypeEnum.Vertical_NoBotHead || vesselType == VesselTypeEnum.Vertical)
			{
				switch (headType)
				{
				case FIREHeadTypeEnum.Ellipsoidal:
					return 0.252 * diameter;
				case FIREHeadTypeEnum.Hemispherical:
					return 0.5 * diameter;
				}
			}
			return 0.0;
		}
	}
	internal interface DisengagementModel
	{
		double BubbleRiseVelocity(double vaporDensity_kg_m3, double liquidDensity_kg_mg3, double liquidSurfaceTension_dyn_cm);

		double OnsetVoidFractionForEntrainment(double vaporDensity_kg_m3, double liquidDensity_kg_m3, double vesselHeight_m, double vesselCrossArea_m2, double dimensionlessSuperficialVelocity);

		double CalculateSurfaceVaporMassFraction(double averageVoidFraction, double vaporDensity_kg_m3, double liquidDensity_kg_m3, double C0);
	}
	internal interface VesselModel
	{
		double OnsetVoidFractionForSwell(double dimensionlessSuperficialVelocity, VesselTypeEnum vesselOrientation, HeadsInFireArea headsIncluded, double C0);
	}
	internal class ChurnTurbulentModel : DisengagementModel
	{
		public double BubbleRiseVelocity(double vaporDensity_kg_m3, double liquidDensity_kg_mg3, double liquidSurfaceTension_dyn_cm)
		{
			double num = liquidSurfaceTension_dyn_cm * 0.001;
			return 1.53 * Math.Pow(num * 9.80665 * (liquidDensity_kg_mg3 - vaporDensity_kg_m3), 0.25) / Math.Sqrt(liquidDensity_kg_mg3);
		}

		double DisengagementModel.BubbleRiseVelocity(double vaporDensity_kg_m3, double liquidDensity_kg_mg3, double liquidSurfaceTension_dyn_cm)
		{
			//ILSpy generated this explicit interface implementation from .override directive in BubbleRiseVelocity
			return this.BubbleRiseVelocity(vaporDensity_kg_m3, liquidDensity_kg_mg3, liquidSurfaceTension_dyn_cm);
		}

		public double OnsetVoidFractionForEntrainment(double vaporDensity_kg_m3, double liquidDensity_kg_m3, double vesselHeight_m, double vesselCrossArea_m2, double dimensionlessSuperficialVelocity)
		{
			return 0.2849 * Math.Sqrt(vesselCrossArea_m2 * dimensionlessSuperficialVelocity * Math.Sqrt(vaporDensity_kg_m3 / liquidDensity_kg_m3)) / vesselHeight_m;
		}

		double DisengagementModel.OnsetVoidFractionForEntrainment(double vaporDensity_kg_m3, double liquidDensity_kg_m3, double vesselHeight_m, double vesselCrossArea_m2, double dimensionlessSuperficialVelocity)
		{
			//ILSpy generated this explicit interface implementation from .override directive in OnsetVoidFractionForEntrainment
			return this.OnsetVoidFractionForEntrainment(vaporDensity_kg_m3, liquidDensity_kg_m3, vesselHeight_m, vesselCrossArea_m2, dimensionlessSuperficialVelocity);
		}

		public double CalculateSurfaceVaporMassFraction(double averageVoidFraction, double vaporDensity_kg_m3, double liquidDensity_kg_m3, double C0)
		{
			return FireScenario._CalculateTwoPhaseVaporFraction(2.0 * averageVoidFraction / (1.0 + C0 * averageVoidFraction), vaporDensity_kg_m3, liquidDensity_kg_m3);
		}

		double DisengagementModel.CalculateSurfaceVaporMassFraction(double averageVoidFraction, double vaporDensity_kg_m3, double liquidDensity_kg_m3, double C0)
		{
			//ILSpy generated this explicit interface implementation from .override directive in CalculateSurfaceVaporMassFraction
			return this.CalculateSurfaceVaporMassFraction(averageVoidFraction, vaporDensity_kg_m3, liquidDensity_kg_m3, C0);
		}
	}
	internal class WallBoilingCTModel : VesselModel
	{
		public double OnsetVoidFractionForSwell(double dimensionlessSuperficialVelocity, VesselTypeEnum vesselOrientation, HeadsInFireArea headsIncluded, double C0)
		{
			switch (vesselOrientation)
			{
			case VesselTypeEnum.Horizontal:
			case VesselTypeEnum.Sphere:
				return dimensionlessSuperficialVelocity / (1.0 + C0 * dimensionlessSuperficialVelocity);
			case VesselTypeEnum.Vertical:
				if (headsIncluded == HeadsInFireArea.BottomOnly || headsIncluded == HeadsInFireArea.Both)
				{
					return dimensionlessSuperficialVelocity / (2.0 + C0 * dimensionlessSuperficialVelocity);
				}
				return dimensionlessSuperficialVelocity / (2.0 + C0 * dimensionlessSuperficialVelocity) / 3.0;
			case VesselTypeEnum.Vertical_BotHead:
				return dimensionlessSuperficialVelocity / (2.0 + C0 * dimensionlessSuperficialVelocity);
			case VesselTypeEnum.Vertical_NoBotHead:
				return dimensionlessSuperficialVelocity / (2.0 + C0 * dimensionlessSuperficialVelocity) / 3.0;
			default:
				throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("vesselOrientation");
			}
		}

		double VesselModel.OnsetVoidFractionForSwell(double dimensionlessSuperficialVelocity, VesselTypeEnum vesselOrientation, HeadsInFireArea headsIncluded, double C0)
		{
			//ILSpy generated this explicit interface implementation from .override directive in OnsetVoidFractionForSwell
			return this.OnsetVoidFractionForSwell(dimensionlessSuperficialVelocity, vesselOrientation, headsIncluded, C0);
		}
	}
	internal class TotalBoilingCTModel : VesselModel
	{
		public double OnsetVoidFractionForSwell(double dimensionlessSuperficialVelocity, VesselTypeEnum vesselOrientation, HeadsInFireArea headsIncluded, double C0)
		{
			return dimensionlessSuperficialVelocity / (2.0 + C0 * dimensionlessSuperficialVelocity);
		}

		double VesselModel.OnsetVoidFractionForSwell(double dimensionlessSuperficialVelocity, VesselTypeEnum vesselOrientation, HeadsInFireArea headsIncluded, double C0)
		{
			//ILSpy generated this explicit interface implementation from .override directive in OnsetVoidFractionForSwell
			return this.OnsetVoidFractionForSwell(dimensionlessSuperficialVelocity, vesselOrientation, headsIncluded, C0);
		}
	}
	internal class BubblyModel : DisengagementModel
	{
		public double BubbleRiseVelocity(double vaporDensity_kg_m3, double liquidDensity_kg_mg3, double liquidSurfaceTension_dyn_cm)
		{
			double num = liquidSurfaceTension_dyn_cm * 0.001;
			return 1.18 * Math.Pow(num * 9.80665 * (liquidDensity_kg_mg3 - vaporDensity_kg_m3), 0.25) / Math.Sqrt(liquidDensity_kg_mg3);
		}

		double DisengagementModel.BubbleRiseVelocity(double vaporDensity_kg_m3, double liquidDensity_kg_mg3, double liquidSurfaceTension_dyn_cm)
		{
			//ILSpy generated this explicit interface implementation from .override directive in BubbleRiseVelocity
			return this.BubbleRiseVelocity(vaporDensity_kg_m3, liquidDensity_kg_mg3, liquidSurfaceTension_dyn_cm);
		}

		public double OnsetVoidFractionForEntrainment(double vaporDensity_kg_m3, double liquidDensity_kg_m3, double vesselHeight_m, double vesselCrossArea_m2, double dimensionlessSuperficialVelocity)
		{
			return 0.2502 * Math.Sqrt(vesselCrossArea_m2 * dimensionlessSuperficialVelocity * Math.Sqrt(vaporDensity_kg_m3 / liquidDensity_kg_m3)) / vesselHeight_m;
		}

		double DisengagementModel.OnsetVoidFractionForEntrainment(double vaporDensity_kg_m3, double liquidDensity_kg_m3, double vesselHeight_m, double vesselCrossArea_m2, double dimensionlessSuperficialVelocity)
		{

			return this.OnsetVoidFractionForEntrainment(vaporDensity_kg_m3, liquidDensity_kg_m3, vesselHeight_m, vesselCrossArea_m2, dimensionlessSuperficialVelocity);
		}

		public double CalculateSurfaceVaporMassFraction(double averageVoidFraction, double vaporDensity_kg_m3, double liquidDensity_kg_m3, double C0)
		{
			return FireScenario._CalculateTwoPhaseVaporFraction(averageVoidFraction, vaporDensity_kg_m3, liquidDensity_kg_m3);
		}

		double DisengagementModel.CalculateSurfaceVaporMassFraction(double averageVoidFraction, double vaporDensity_kg_m3, double liquidDensity_kg_m3, double C0)
		{
			//ILSpy generated this explicit interface implementation from .override directive in CalculateSurfaceVaporMassFraction
			return this.CalculateSurfaceVaporMassFraction(averageVoidFraction, vaporDensity_kg_m3, liquidDensity_kg_m3, C0);
		}

	internal class BubblyVesselModel : VesselModel
	{
		private double _OnsetPsi(double voidFraction, double C0)
		{
			return voidFraction * Math.Pow(1.0 - voidFraction, 2.0) / ((1.0 - Math.Pow(voidFraction, 3.0)) * (1.0 - C0 * voidFraction));
		}

		public double OnsetVoidFractionForSwell(double dimensionlessSuperficialVelocity, VesselTypeEnum vesselOrientation, HeadsInFireArea headsIncluded, double C0)
		{
			if (_OnsetPsi(5.0 / 6.0, C0) < dimensionlessSuperficialVelocity)
			{
				return 5.0 / 6.0;
			}
			return RootFinder.RootFinder([SpecialName] (double alpha) => dimensionlessSuperficialVelocity - _OnsetPsi(alpha, C0), 0.5, 0.0, 5.0 / 6.0);
		}

		double VesselModel.OnsetVoidFractionForSwell(double dimensionlessSuperficialVelocity, VesselTypeEnum vesselOrientation, HeadsInFireArea headsIncluded, double C0)
		{
			//ILSpy generated this explicit interface implementation from .override directive in OnsetVoidFractionForSwell
			return this.OnsetVoidFractionForSwell(dimensionlessSuperficialVelocity, vesselOrientation, headsIncluded, C0);
		}
	}

	internal class SphericalVessel : VesselGeometry
	{
		public double Diameter_m { get; }

		public double Height_m => Diameter_m;

		{
			Diameter_m = double.NaN;
			Diameter_m = diameter_m;
		}

		public double CalculateCrossSectionalArea_m2()
		{
			return Math.PI / 4.0 * Diameter_m * Diameter_m;
		}

		double VesselGeometry.CalculateCrossSectionalArea_m2()
		{
			//ILSpy generated this explicit interface implementation from .override directive in CalculateCrossSectionalArea_m2
			return this.CalculateCrossSectionalArea_m2();
		}
	}
	internal class HorizontalVessel : VesselGeometry
	{
		public double Diameter_m { get; }

		public double Length_m { get; }

		public double Height_m => Diameter_m;

		public HorizontalVessel(double diameter_m, double length_m)
		{
			Diameter_m = double.NaN;
			Length_m = double.NaN;
			Diameter_m = diameter_m;
			Length_m = length_m;
		}

		public double CalculateCrossSectionalArea_m2()
		{
			return Diameter_m * Length_m;
		}
