	[StandardModule]
	public sealed class FluidExtensions
	{
		public static bool ConstantDensityFlashByT(this IPPPFluid @this, double pressure_kPa, double density_kgm3)
		{
			double errorTol = 0.01;
			double num = @this.Temperature();
			double xmin = Math.Max(num - 500.0, -273.0);
			double xmax = 1000000.0;
			double initialStepSize = 10.0;
			double value = 30.0;
			double num2 = double.NaN;
			bool result;
			try
			{
				num2 = RootFinder.RootFinder([SpecialName] (double T) =>
				{
					@this.TPFlashOverall(T, pressure_kPa);
					return @this.OverallDensity() - density_kgm3;
				}, num, xmin, xmax, initialStepSize, value, errorTol, AllowConvergedOutsideTolerance: false);
			}
			catch (RootFinder.RootFailedException ex)
			{
				ProjectData.SetProjectError(ex);
				RootFinder.RootFailedException ex2 = ex;
				result = false;
				ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
				goto IL_00c2;
			}
			result = @this.TPFlash(num2, pressure_kPa);
			goto IL_00c2;
			IL_00c2:
			return result;
		}

		public static bool ConstantDensityFlashByH(this IPPPFluid @this, double pressure_kPa, double density_kgm3, double lowerBound_kJkmol = double.NegativeInfinity, double upperBound_kJkmol = double.PositiveInfinity)
		{
			double errorTol = 0.01;
			double xguess = OverallMolarEnthalpy(@this);
			double num = double.NaN;
			bool result;
			try
			{
				num = RootFinder.RootFinder([SpecialName] (double H) =>
				{
					@this.PHFlash(pressure_kPa, H);
					return @this.OverallDensity() - density_kgm3;
				}, xguess, lowerBound_kJkmol, upperBound_kJkmol, 0.0, null, errorTol);
			}
			catch (RootFinder.RootFailedException ex)
			{
				ProjectData.SetProjectError(ex);
				RootFinder.RootFailedException ex2 = ex;
				result = false;
				ProjectData.ClearProjectError();
				goto IL_008f;
			}
			result = @this.PHFlash(pressure_kPa, num);
			goto IL_008f;
			IL_008f:
			return result;
		}

		public static bool ConstantDensityFlash(this IPPPFluid @this, double pressure_kPa, double density_kgm3, [Optional][DefaultParameterValue(true)] ref bool canFlashByTemperature, double lowerBoundEnthalpy_kJkmol = double.NegativeInfinity, double upperBoundEnthalpy_kJkmol = double.PositiveInfinity)
		{
			bool flag = false;
			if (canFlashByTemperature)
			{
				double h = OverallMolarEnthalpy(@this);
				flag = ConstantDensityFlashByT(@this, pressure_kPa, density_kgm3);
				if (!flag)
				{
					@this.PHFlash(pressure_kPa, h);
				}
			}
			bool flag2 = false;
			if (!flag)
			{
				flag2 = ConstantDensityFlashByH(@this, pressure_kPa, density_kgm3, lowerBoundEnthalpy_kJkmol, upperBoundEnthalpy_kJkmol);
				if (flag2 & canFlashByTemperature)
				{
					canFlashByTemperature = false;
				}
			}
			return flag || flag2;
		}

		public static double CalculateLatentHeat(this IPPPFluid @this, double initialVapFn, double finalVapFn, bool includeSensibleHeat = true)
		{
			double num = @this.Pressure();
			double h = @this.OverallMassEnthalpy() * @this.OverallMolecularWeight();
			double result = double.NaN;
			try
			{
				if (!(initialVapFn > 0.0) || @this.PVFlashLiquid(num, initialVapFn))
				{
					double num2 = @this.OverallMassEnthalpy();
					double num3 = @this.OverallMassHeatCapacity();
					double num4 = @this.Temperature();
					double num5 = @this.VaporMassFraction();
					if (@this.PVFlash(num, finalVapFn))
					{
						double num6 = @this.OverallMassEnthalpy();
						double num7 = 0.0;
						double num8 = @this.VaporMassFraction();
						if (!includeSensibleHeat)
						{
							double num9 = @this.Temperature();
							num7 = num3 * (num9 - num4);
						}
						result = (num6 - num2 - num7) / (num8 - num5);
					}
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				@this.PHFlash(num, h);
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static double OverallMolarEnthalpy(this IPPPFluid @this)
		{
			return @this.OverallMassEnthalpy() * @this.OverallMolecularWeight();
		}

		public static double VaporVolumeFraction(this IPPPFluid @this)
		{
			if (double.IsNaN(@this.VaporMassFraction()))
			{
				return double.NaN;
			}
			if (@this.VaporMassFraction() == 0.0)
			{
				return 0.0;
			}
			double num = @this.VaporMassFraction() / @this.VaporMassDensity();
			double num2 = 1.0 / @this.OverallDensity();
			return num / num2;
		}

		public static bool IsSingleComponentStream(this IPPPFluid @this)
		{
			int num = @this.NumberOfComponents();
			if (num == 1)
			{
				return true;
			}
			checked
			{
				int num2 = num - 1;
				for (int i = 0; i <= num2; i++)
				{
					double num3 = @this.get_Composition(i);
					if (num3 > 0.0 && num3 < 1.0)
					{
						return false;
					}
				}
				return true;
			}
		}
	}
	public class LineSizing
	{
		private static readonly Dictionary<AIVVaporFraction, string> _FractionTypeMap = new Dictionary<AIVVaporFraction, string>
		{
			{
				AIVVaporFraction.Mass,
				Resources.Mass
			},
			{
				AIVVaporFraction.Mole,
				Resources.Mole
			},
			{
				AIVVaporFraction.Volume,
				Resources.Volume
			}
		};

		public static CalculationStatusEnum VaporSizeLines(ref double InPressDrop, ref double InAvgDensity, ref double InAvgVelocity, ref double OutPressDrop, ref double OutAvgDensity, ref double OutAvgVelocity, ref double OutExitVelocity, ref double CritVel, ref double CriticPress, ref bool ChokedFlag, double InID, double InEquivLength, double InRough, double OutID, double OutEquivLength, double OutRough, double UpRatedFlow, double RelievingTemp, double SetPress, double SpecHeatRatio, double Compress, double MolWeight, double FluidVisc, double SuperImpConstBackPress, double SuperImpVarBackPress, double VarBackPress, ref string returnMsg)
		{
			CalculationStatusEnum calculationStatusEnum = CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationOK;
			CritVel = Math.Pow(SpecHeatRatio * 8314.0 * (RelievingTemp + 273.15) / MolWeight, 0.5);
			CriticPress = 3.23E-05 * (UpRatedFlow / Math.Pow(OutID * 0.0254, 2.0)) * Math.Pow(Compress * (RelievingTemp + 273.15) / (SpecHeatRatio * MolWeight), 0.5) / 100.0;
			double num = 3.1416 * Math.Pow(InID * 0.0254, 2.0) / 4.0;
			InAvgDensity = SetPress / 1.013 * MolWeight / 0.0805 / (RelievingTemp + 273.15) / Compress;
			InAvgVelocity = UpRatedFlow / InAvgDensity / num / 3600.0;
			double num2 = ValveUtilities.FrictionFactor(InAvgVelocity * InAvgDensity * (InID * 0.0254) / (FluidVisc / 1000.0), InRough, InID);
			InPressDrop = InEquivLength / 100.0 * (num2 * Math.Pow(InAvgVelocity, 2.0) * InAvgDensity / (12.6988 * InID));
			double num3 = 3.1416 * Math.Pow(OutID * 0.0254, 2.0) / 4.0;
			double num4 = SuperImpConstBackPress + SuperImpVarBackPress + VarBackPress;
			int num5 = 0;
			double num10;
			do
			{
				CriticPress = num4 * Math.Pow(2.0 / (SpecHeatRatio + 1.0), SpecHeatRatio / (SpecHeatRatio - 1.0));
				double num6 = SuperImpConstBackPress + SuperImpVarBackPress;
				if (CriticPress >= num6)
				{
					num6 = CriticPress;
					ChokedFlag = true;
				}
				else
				{
					ChokedFlag = false;
				}
				double num7 = num4 / 1.013 * MolWeight / 0.0805 / (RelievingTemp + 273.15);
				double num8 = num6 / 1.013 * MolWeight / 0.0805 / (RelievingTemp + 273.15);
				OutAvgDensity = (num7 + num8) / 2.0;
				OutExitVelocity = UpRatedFlow / num8 / num3 / 3600.0;
				OutAvgVelocity = UpRatedFlow / OutAvgDensity / num3 / 3600.0;
				double num9 = ValveUtilities.FrictionFactor(OutAvgVelocity * OutAvgDensity * (OutID * 0.0254) / (FluidVisc / 1000.0), OutRough, OutID);
				OutPressDrop = OutEquivLength / 100.0 * (num9 * Math.Pow(OutAvgVelocity, 2.0) * OutAvgDensity / (12.6988 * OutID));
				num10 = num6 + OutPressDrop;
				if (Math.Abs(num10 - num4) > 0.05)
				{
					num4 = ((!(num4 - num10 < 0.0)) ? (num4 - 0.01) : (num4 + 0.01));
				}
				num5 = checked(num5 + 1);
				if (num5 > 50000)
				{
					returnMsg = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("TooManyIter");
					calculationStatusEnum = CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationError;
					break;
				}
			}
			while (Math.Abs(num10 - num4) > 0.05);
			if (calculationStatusEnum != CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationError)
			{
				if (ChokedFlag)
				{
					OutPressDrop += CriticPress - SuperImpConstBackPress - SuperImpVarBackPress;
					OutExitVelocity = CritVel;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				OutPressDrop = double.NaN;
			}
			return calculationStatusEnum;
		}

		public static double CalculateReactionForce(double RelievingFlow, double RelievingTemp, double CriticPress, double SuperImpConstBackPress, double SuperImpVarBackPress, double AtmPress, double OutID, double SpecHeatRatio, double MolWeight)
		{
			double num = 3.1416 * Math.Pow(OutID * 0.0254, 2.0) / 4.0;
			double num2 = SuperImpConstBackPress + SuperImpVarBackPress;
			if (CriticPress >= num2)
			{
				num2 = CriticPress;
			}
			return 129.0 * RelievingFlow / 3600.0 * Math.Pow(SpecHeatRatio * (RelievingTemp + 273.15) / ((SpecHeatRatio + 1.0) * MolWeight), 0.5) + 0.1 * num * 1000000.0 * (num2 - AtmPress);
		}

		public static CalculationStatusEnum LiquidSizeLines(ref double InPressDrop, ref double InAvgVelocity, ref double OutPressDrop, ref double OutAvgVelocity, double InID, double InAvgDensity, double InEquivLength, double InRough, double OutID, double OutAvgDensity, double OutRough, double OutEquivLength, double UpratedFlow, double FluidVisc, ref string returnMsg)
		{
			double num = 3.1416 * Math.Pow(InID * 0.0254, 2.0) / 4.0;
			InAvgVelocity = UpratedFlow / InAvgDensity / num / 3600.0;
			double num2 = ValveUtilities.FrictionFactor(InAvgVelocity * InAvgDensity * (InID * 0.0254) / (FluidVisc / 1000.0), InRough, InID);
			InPressDrop = InEquivLength / 100.0 * (num2 * Math.Pow(InAvgVelocity, 2.0) * InAvgDensity / (12.6988 * InID));
			double num3 = 3.1416 * Math.Pow(OutID * 0.0254, 2.0) / 4.0;
			OutAvgVelocity = UpratedFlow / OutAvgDensity / num3 / 3600.0;
			double num4 = ValveUtilities.FrictionFactor(OutAvgVelocity * OutAvgDensity * (OutID * 0.0254) / (FluidVisc / 1000.0), OutRough, OutID);
			OutPressDrop = OutEquivLength / 100.0 * (num4 * Math.Pow(OutAvgVelocity, 2.0) * OutAvgDensity / (12.6988 * OutID));
			return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationOK;
		}

		public static CalculationStatusEnum CalculateTwoPhaseLine_Leung(ref double INK_in, ref double Ph2InEquivLength, ref double Ph2OutEquivLength, ref bool Ph2Out_isChoked, double Ph2InID, double Ph2OutID, double INK_pipe, double Ph2INK_Entrance, double G, double Po, double vo, double Kd, double A_n, double Ps, double W, double Omega, double SuperImpConstBackPress, double SuperImpVarBackPress, double VarBackPress, ref string returnMsg)
		{
			double num = 3.14;
			CalculationStatusEnum result = CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationOK;
			double num2 = num * Math.Pow(Ph2InID, 2.0) / 4.0 * 0.00064516;
			if (num2 == 0.0)
			{
				returnMsg = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("PipeDiam");
				return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationError;
			}
			double num3 = G / Math.Pow(Po / vo, 0.5);
			INK_in = 2.0 * Math.Pow(0.9 / (Kd * num3), 2.0) / Math.Pow(A_n / num2, 2.0) * (0.03 * (1.0 - 101353.0 / Ps) / (1.1 * (1.0 - 101353.0 / Ps) + 101353.0 / Ps));
			INK_pipe = INK_in - Ph2INK_Entrance;
			Ph2InEquivLength = INK_pipe * Ph2InID / 12.0 / 0.02;
			if (Ph2InEquivLength < 0.0)
			{
				returnMsg = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("PipeLength");
				return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationError;
			}
			double num4 = num * Math.Pow(Ph2OutID, 2.0) / 4.0 * 0.00064516;
			double num5 = W / num4 * Math.Pow(Po * Omega * vo, 0.5) * 0.000145038;
			double num6 = SuperImpConstBackPress + SuperImpVarBackPress;
			if (num5 > num6)
			{
				num6 = num5;
				Ph2Out_isChoked = true;
			}
			else
			{
				Ph2Out_isChoked = false;
			}
			double num7 = num6 + VarBackPress;
			if (num7 > SuperImpConstBackPress + SuperImpVarBackPress + VarBackPress)
			{
				returnMsg = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BackHigh");
				result = CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationWarning;
			}
			Po *= 0.000145038;
			double num8 = num7 / Po;
			double num9 = num6 / Po;
			Po /= 0.000145038;
			Ph2OutEquivLength = Ph2OutID / 12.0 / 0.02 * (2.0 * Po * (1.0 / vo) / Math.Pow(W / num4, 2.0) * ((num8 - num9) / (1.0 - Omega) + Omega / Math.Pow(1.0 - Omega, 2.0) * Math.Log(((1.0 - Omega) * num9 + Omega) / ((1.0 - Omega) * num8 + Omega))) - 2.0 * Math.Log(((1.0 - Omega) * num9 + Omega) / ((1.0 - Omega) * num8 + Omega) * num8 / num9));
			return result;
		}

		public static CalculationStatusEnum CalculateTwoPhaseLine_Homogeneous(ref double PressDrop, ref double AvgVel, ref double AvgRhoV2, double VapFract, double GasDens, double LiqDens, double ID, double Flowrate, double ViscGas, double ViscLiq, double EquivLength, double Rough)
		{
			double num = ((VapFract < 0.001) ? 0.0 : ((!(VapFract > 0.99)) ? (1.0 / (1.0 + (1.0 - VapFract) / VapFract * GasDens / LiqDens)) : 1.0));
			double num2 = LiqDens * (1.0 - num) + GasDens * num;
			double num3 = 3.1416 * Math.Pow(ID * 0.0254, 2.0) / 4.0;
			double num4 = Flowrate / 3600.0 / num3;
			double num5 = (VapFract * ViscGas + (1.0 - VapFract) * ViscLiq) * 0.001;
			double reynolds = num4 * (ID * 0.0254) / num5;
			AvgVel = Flowrate / num2 / num3 / 3600.0;
			AvgRhoV2 = num2 * Math.Pow(AvgVel, 2.0);
			double num6 = ValveUtilities.FrictionFactor(reynolds, Rough, ID);
			PressDrop = EquivLength / 100.0 * (num6 * Math.Pow(AvgVel, 2.0) * num2 / (12.6988 * ID));
			return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationOK;
		}

		public static double LinesPressDrop(double ID, double SetPress, double MolWeight, double RelievingTemp, double Compress, double RatedFlow, double FluidVisc, double EquivLength, double Rough, double SpecHeatRatio)
		{
			double num = 3.1416 * Math.Pow(ID * 0.0254, 2.0) / 4.0;
			Math.Pow(SpecHeatRatio * 8314.0 * (RelievingTemp + 273.15) / MolWeight, 0.5);
			double num2 = SetPress / 1.013 * MolWeight / 0.0805 / (RelievingTemp + 273.15) / Compress;
			double num3 = RatedFlow / num2 / num / 3600.0;
			double num4 = ValveUtilities.FrictionFactor(num3 * num2 * (ID * 0.0254) / (FluidVisc / 1000.0), Rough, ID);
			return EquivLength / 100.0 * (num4 * Math.Pow(num3, 2.0) * num2 / (12.6988 * ID));
		}

		public static CalculationStatusEnum CalculateSourceSoundPowerLevel(ref double CalcSPL, double RelievingFlow_kg_h, IPPPFluid RelievingFluid, double TotalBackpressure_bara, AIVMixedPhaseOption MixedPhaseOption, AIVVaporFraction VaporFractionOption, double VaporFractionLimit, ref string msg)
		{
			CalcSPL = double.NaN;
			if (RelievingFluid.VaporFraction() < 1.0 && MixedPhaseOption == AIVMixedPhaseOption.IgnoreAll)
			{
				msg = Resources.MixedSourceIgnored;
				return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationOK;
			}
			if (RelievingFluid.VaporFraction() == 0.0 && MixedPhaseOption == AIVMixedPhaseOption.VaporPhaseOnly)
			{
				msg = Resources.LiquidSourceIgnored;
				return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationOK;
			}
			if (MixedPhaseOption == AIVMixedPhaseOption.IgnoreByVaporFraction)
			{
				switch (VaporFractionOption)
				{
				case AIVVaporFraction.Mole:
					if (RelievingFluid.VaporFraction() < VaporFractionLimit)
					{
						msg = string.Format(Resources.MixedSourceUnderLimitIgnored, _FractionTypeMap[VaporFractionOption], RelievingFluid.VaporFraction() * 100.0, VaporFractionLimit * 100.0);
						return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationOK;
					}
					break;
				case AIVVaporFraction.Mass:
					if (RelievingFluid.VaporMassFraction() < VaporFractionLimit)
					{
						msg = string.Format(Resources.MixedSourceUnderLimitIgnored, _FractionTypeMap[VaporFractionOption], RelievingFluid.VaporMassFraction() * 100.0, VaporFractionLimit * 100.0);
						return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationOK;
					}
					break;
				case AIVVaporFraction.Volume:
				{
					double num = double.NaN;
					double num2 = double.NaN;
					if (RelievingFluid.VaporFraction() > 0.0)
					{
						num2 = RelievingFluid.VaporMassDensity();
						if (double.IsNaN(num2) || num2 == 0.0)
						{
							msg = Resources.SPLErrorMissingVaporDensity;
							return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationError;
						}
					}
					else
					{
						num = 0.0;
					}
					double num3 = double.NaN;
					if (RelievingFluid.VaporFraction() < 1.0)
					{
						num3 = RelievingFluid.OverallDensity();
						if (double.IsNaN(num3) || num3 == 0.0)
						{
							msg = Resources.SPLErrorMissingOverallDensity;
							return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationError;
						}
					}
					else
					{
						num = 1.0;
					}
					if (double.IsNaN(num))
					{
						double num4 = 1.0 / num3;
						num = RelievingFluid.VaporMassFraction() / num2 / num4;
					}
					if (num < VaporFractionLimit)
					{
						msg = string.Format(Resources.MixedSourceUnderLimitIgnored, _FractionTypeMap[VaporFractionOption], num * 100.0, VaporFractionLimit * 100.0);
						return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationOK;
					}
					break;
				}
				}
			}
			double num5 = RelievingFluid.OverallMolecularWeight();
			if (MixedPhaseOption == AIVMixedPhaseOption.VaporPhaseOnly)
			{
				RelievingFlow_kg_h *= RelievingFluid.VaporMassFraction();
				num5 = RelievingFluid.VaporMolecularWeight();
			}
			double num6 = RelievingFluid.Pressure();
			double num7 = TotalBackpressure_bara * 100.0;
			double num8 = RelievingFluid.Temperature() + 273.15;
			if (num7 > num6)
			{
				msg = Resources.SPLErrorExcessiveBackpressure;
				return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationError;
			}
			double d = Math.Pow(RelievingFlow_kg_h * 0.0002777777777777778, 2.0) * Math.Pow((num6 - num7) / num6, 3.6) * Math.Pow(num8 / num5, 1.2);
			CalcSPL = 10.0 * Math.Log10(d) + 126.0;
			return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationOK;
		}
	}
	public class LiquidRelief
	{
		public static CalculationStatusEnum CalculateOrificeSize(ref double CalcOrifice, ref double Coeff_Kw, ref double Coeff_Kv, double RelievingFlow, ValveTypeEnum ValveType, Api520Part1Edition ApiEdition, double Coeff_Kc, double Coeff_Kd, bool calculatedKw, double SetPress, double OverPress, double TotBackPress, double AtmPress, double InletLoss, double SpecGravity, double FluidVisc, double LiftRestriction, double[] AvailOrifices, ref string txtMsg)
		{
			double Coeff_Kp = 1.0;
			return _CalculateOrificeSizeImpl(ref CalcOrifice, ref Coeff_Kw, ref Coeff_Kv, ref Coeff_Kp, isCapacityCertified: true, RelievingFlow, ValveType, ApiEdition, Coeff_Kc, Coeff_Kd, calculatedKw, calculatedKp: false, SetPress, OverPress, TotBackPress, AtmPress, InletLoss, SpecGravity, FluidVisc, LiftRestriction, AvailOrifices, ref txtMsg);
		}

		public static CalculationStatusEnum CalculateOrificeSizeNonCertified(ref double CalcOrifice, ref double Coeff_Kw, ref double Coeff_Kp, ref double Coeff_Kv, double RelievingFlow, ValveTypeEnum ValveType, Api520Part1Edition ApiEdition, double Coeff_Kc, double Coeff_Kd, bool calculatedKw, bool calculatedKp, double SetPress, double OverPress, double TotBackPress, double AtmPress, double InletLoss, double SpecGravity, double FluidVisc, double LiftRestriction, double[] AvailOrifices, ref string txtMsg)
		{
			return _CalculateOrificeSizeImpl(ref CalcOrifice, ref Coeff_Kw, ref Coeff_Kv, ref Coeff_Kp, isCapacityCertified: false, RelievingFlow, ValveType, ApiEdition, Coeff_Kc, Coeff_Kd, calculatedKw, calculatedKp, SetPress, OverPress, TotBackPress, AtmPress, InletLoss, SpecGravity, FluidVisc, LiftRestriction, AvailOrifices, ref txtMsg);
		}

		private static CalculationStatusEnum _CalculateOrificeSizeImpl(ref double CalcOrifice, ref double Coeff_Kw, ref double Coeff_Kv, ref double Coeff_Kp, bool isCapacityCertified, double RelievingFlow, ValveTypeEnum ValveType, Api520Part1Edition ApiEdition, double Coeff_Kc, double Coeff_Kd, bool calculatedKw, bool calculatedKp, double SetPress, double OverPress, double TotBackPress, double AtmPress, double InletLoss, double SpecGravity, double FluidVisc, double LiftRestriction, double[] AvailOrifices, ref string txtMsg)
		{
			CalculationStatusEnum result = CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationOK;
			double num = double.NaN;
			num = ((!isCapacityCertified) ? (1.25 * (SetPress - AtmPress) + AtmPress) : ((SetPress - AtmPress) * (1.0 + OverPress / 100.0) + AtmPress));
			if (!double.IsNaN(InletLoss))
			{
				num -= InletLoss;
			}
			Coeff_Kv = 1.0;
			double num2 = SpecGravity * 62.4;
			if (calculatedKw)
			{
				Coeff_Kw = ValveUtilities.CalculateKw(ValveType, SetPress, AtmPress, TotBackPress);
			}
			if (isCapacityCertified)
			{
				Coeff_Kp = 1.0;
			}
			else if (calculatedKp)
			{
				Coeff_Kp = ValveUtilities.CalculateKp(OverPress);
			}
			if (RelievingFlow == 0.0)
			{
				CalcOrifice = 0.0;
				return result;
			}
			double num3 = RelievingFlow / num2 / 8.020833333333334;
			CalcOrifice = num3 / 38.0 / Coeff_Kd / Coeff_Kw / Coeff_Kc / Coeff_Kv / Coeff_Kp * Math.Pow(SpecGravity / (num - TotBackPress), 0.5);
			checked
			{
				double num5 = default(double);
				do
				{
					bool flag = false;
					int num4 = AvailOrifices.Length - 1;
					for (int i = 0; i <= num4; i++)
					{
						if (CalcOrifice < LiftRestriction * AvailOrifices[i])
						{
							num5 = LiftRestriction * AvailOrifices[i];
							flag = true;
							break;
						}
					}
					if (!flag)
					{
						Coeff_Kv = 1.0;
						num5 = double.PositiveInfinity;
						txtMsg = Resources.LiquidKvSelectedOrifice;
						result = CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationWarning;
					}
					else
					{
						double num6 = num3 * 2800.0 * SpecGravity / FluidVisc / Math.Pow(num5, 0.5);
						if (ApiEdition == Api520Part1Edition.Edition9)
						{
							Coeff_Kv = Math.Pow(0.9935 + 2.878 / Math.Pow(num6, 0.5) + 342.0 / Math.Pow(num6, 1.5), -1.0);
						}
						else if (FluidVisc > 100.0)
						{
							if (num6 >= 80.0)
							{
								Coeff_Kv = 1.0 / Math.Sqrt(170.0 / num6 + 1.0);
							}
							else
							{
								Coeff_Kv = 1.0;
								num5 = double.PositiveInfinity;
								txtMsg = Resources.LiquidKvReTooLow;
								result = CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationWarning;
							}
						}
						else
						{
							Coeff_Kv = 1.0;
							num5 = double.PositiveInfinity;
						}
					}
					CalcOrifice = num3 / 38.0 / Coeff_Kd / Coeff_Kw / Coeff_Kc / Coeff_Kv / Coeff_Kp * Math.Pow(SpecGravity / (num - TotBackPress), 0.5);
				}
				while (CalcOrifice > num5);
				return result;
			}
		}
	}
	public class Mixed_Relief
	{
		public struct DirectIntegrationStep
		{
			public double Pressure;

			public double Temperature;

			public double VapQualMass;

			public double Density;

			public double Integrand;

			public double Summation;

			public double MassFlux;
		}

		public static CalculationStatusEnum CalculateOrificeSize_HDIShortcut(ref double calcOrifice_in2, ref double calcMassFlux_lbhin2, double relievingFlow_lbh, double relievingTemp_R, double relievingVf, ValveTypeEnum valveType, ref double coeffKb, double coeffKc, double coeffKd, double coeffKv, bool calculateKb, double setPressure_psia, double overPress, double atmPressure_psia, double totalBackpressure_psia, double upstreamPressure_psia, IPPPFluid fluid, bool isFireCalc, bool shouldReflash, ref string returnMsg)
		{
			CalculationStatusEnum calculationStatusEnum = CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationOK;
			double p = upstreamPressure_psia * 6.89475908677537;
			double t = relievingTemp_R * (5.0 / 9.0) + -273.15;
			if (shouldReflash)
			{
				bool flag = false;
				if (!isFireCalc)
				{
					flag = fluid.TPFlash(t, p);
					if (flag && !double.IsNaN(relievingVf))
					{
						bool flag2 = false;
						if (relievingVf == 0.0 || relievingVf == 1.0)
						{
							if (relievingVf != fluid.VaporFraction())
							{
								flag2 = true;
							}
						}
						else if (Math.Abs(relievingVf - fluid.VaporFraction()) / relievingVf > 0.0001)
						{
							flag2 = true;
						}
						if (flag2)
						{
							flag = fluid.PVFlash(p, relievingVf);
						}
					}
				}
				else
				{
					flag = fluid.PVFlashFire(p, relievingVf);
				}
				if (!flag)
				{
					returnMsg = Resources.DirectFlashFailed;
					return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationError;
				}
			}
			double massFlux_kghcm = double.NaN;
			double Pthroat_kPa = double.NaN;
			double totalBackPressure_kPa = totalBackpressure_psia * 6.89475908677537;
			calculationStatusEnum = _CalculateMaxMassFlux(ref massFlux_kghcm, ref Pthroat_kPa, fluid, totalBackPressure_kPa, ref returnMsg);
			if (calculationStatusEnum == CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationError)
			{
				return calculationStatusEnum;
			}
			double criticPress = Pthroat_kPa * 0.1450377;
			if (calculateKb)
			{
				coeffKb = ValveUtilities.CalculateKb(valveType, setPressure_psia, overPress, atmPressure_psia, totalBackpressure_psia, 50.0, criticPress, ref returnMsg);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(returnMsg))
				{
					calculationStatusEnum = CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationWarning;
				}
			}
			double num = massFlux_kghcm * 0.56893305568;
			calcOrifice_in2 = 0.04 * relievingFlow_lbh / (coeffKd * coeffKb * coeffKc * coeffKv * num);
			calcMassFlux_lbhin2 = massFlux_kghcm * 2.20462 / 0.15500031000061998;
			return calculationStatusEnum;
		}

		private static double _CalculateMassFlux(IPPPFluid fluid, double Pthroat_kPa, double Href_kJkg)
		{
			fluid.IsentropicFlash(Pthroat_kPa);
			double num = fluid.OverallMassEnthalpy();
			double num2 = 1.0 / fluid.OverallDensity();
			double num3 = Math.Sqrt(2000.0 * (Href_kJkg - num)) / num2 * 0.36;
			return 0.0 - num3;
		}

		private static CalculationStatusEnum _CalculateMaxMassFlux(ref double massFlux_kghcm2, ref double Pthroat_kPa, IPPPFluid fluid, double totalBackPressure_kPa, ref string returnMsg)
		{
			double href_kJkg = fluid.OverallMassEnthalpy();
			double h = FluidExtensions.OverallMolarEnthalpy(fluid);
			double num = fluid.Pressure();
			double xVal = double.NaN;
			double objVal = double.NaN;
			if (!BrentOptimize.BrentMinimize([SpecialName] (double P, double aux) => _CalculateMassFlux(fluid, P, href_kJkg), totalBackPressure_kPa, num, 0.05, 25, ref xVal, ref objVal, 0.0))
			{
				returnMsg = Resources.MaxMassFluxIterReached;
				return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationError;
			}
			Pthroat_kPa = xVal;
			massFlux_kghcm2 = 0.0 - objVal;
			fluid.PHFlash(num, h);
			return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationOK;
		}

		public static CalculationStatusEnum CalculateOrificeSize_1(ref double CalcOrifice, ref double MassFlux, ref double CriticPress, ref double UpstrPress, ref double Omega, ref double Eta_calc, ref bool flowType_isCritical, double RelievingFlow, double RelievingTemp, ValveTypeEnum ValveType, ref double Coeff_Kb, double Coeff_Kc, double Coeff_Kd, bool calculateKb, double SetPress, double LowSetPressLimit, double OverPress, double AtmPress, double InletLoss, double TotBackPress, double SpecHeatRatio, short opt_150_State, double xo, double V_vo, double V_lo, double cp, double vo, double h_vlo, double v9, ref string returnMsg)
		{
			CalculationStatusEnum calcStatus = CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationOK;
			UpstrPress = (SetPress - AtmPress) * (1.0 + OverPress / 100.0) + AtmPress;
			if (!double.IsNaN(InletLoss))
			{
				UpstrPress -= InletLoss;
			}
			switch (opt_150_State)
			{
			case 1:
			{
				if (xo <= 0.0 || xo > 1.0)
				{
					returnMsg = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("Badxo");
					return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationError;
				}
				double num = V_vo - V_lo;
				Omega = xo * V_vo / (vo * SpecHeatRatio) + 0.185 * cp * RelievingTemp * UpstrPress / vo * Math.Pow(num / h_vlo, 2.0);
				break;
			}
			case 2:
				Omega = 9.0 * (v9 / vo - 1.0);
				break;
			default:
				Omega = xo * V_vo / (vo * SpecHeatRatio);
				if (xo <= 0.0 || xo > 1.0)
				{
					returnMsg = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("Badxo");
					return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationError;
				}
				break;
			}
			if ((Omega < 0.01) | (Omega > 100.0))
			{
				returnMsg = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BadOmega");
				return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationError;
			}
			if (double.IsNaN(Omega))
			{
				returnMsg = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("OtherBadOmega");
				return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationError;
			}
			Eta_calc = Eta_c(Omega, ref returnMsg, ref calcStatus);
			CriticPress = Eta_calc * UpstrPress;
			if (calculateKb)
			{
				Coeff_Kb = ValveUtilities.CalculateKb(ValveType, SetPress, OverPress, AtmPress, TotBackPress, LowSetPressLimit, CriticPress, ref returnMsg);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(returnMsg))
				{
					calcStatus = CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationWarning;
				}
			}
			if (TotBackPress <= CriticPress)
			{
				flowType_isCritical = true;
				MassFlux = 68.09 * Eta_calc * Math.Pow(UpstrPress / (vo * Omega), 0.5);
				CalcOrifice = 0.04 * RelievingFlow / (Coeff_Kd * Coeff_Kb * Coeff_Kc * MassFlux);
			}
			else
			{
				flowType_isCritical = false;
				double num2 = TotBackPress / UpstrPress;
				MassFlux = 68.09 * Math.Pow(-2.0 * (Omega * Math.Log(num2) + (Omega - 1.0) * (1.0 - num2)), 0.5) / (Omega * (1.0 / num2 - 1.0) + 1.0) * Math.Pow(UpstrPress / vo, 0.5);
				if (ValveType == ValveTypeEnum.BalancedBellows)
				{
					CalcOrifice = 0.04 * RelievingFlow / (Coeff_Kd * Coeff_Kb * Coeff_Kc * MassFlux);
				}
				else
				{
					CalcOrifice = 0.04 * RelievingFlow / (Coeff_Kd * Coeff_Kb * Coeff_Kc * MassFlux);
				}
			}
			return calcStatus;
		}

		public static CalculationStatusEnum CalculateOrificeSize_2(ref double CalcOrifice, ref double MassFlux, ref double CriticPress, ref double UpstrPress, ref double Omega_s, ref double Eta_c, ref bool flowType_isCritical, double RelievingFlow, double RelievingTemp, ValveTypeEnum ValveType, ref double Coeff_Kb, double Coeff_Kc, double Coeff_Kd, bool calculateKb, double SetPress, double OverPress, double AtmPress, double InletLoss, double TotBackPress, double LowSetPressLimit, double SpecGravity, short opt_150_state, double V_vs, double V_ls, double cp, double Ps, double h_vls, double Rho_L9, ref string returnMsg)
		{
			CalculationStatusEnum status = CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationOK;
			UpstrPress = (SetPress - AtmPress) * (1.0 + OverPress / 100.0) + AtmPress;
			if (!double.IsNaN(InletLoss))
			{
				UpstrPress -= InletLoss;
			}
			double num = SpecGravity * 62.428;
			switch (opt_150_state)
			{
			case 1:
			{
				double num2 = V_vs - V_ls;
				Omega_s = 0.185 * num * cp * RelievingTemp * Ps * Math.Pow(num2 / h_vls, 2.0);
				break;
			}
			case 2:
				Omega_s = 9.0 * (num / Rho_L9 - 1.0);
				break;
			}
			if ((Omega_s < 0.0) | (Omega_s > 100.0))
			{
				returnMsg = Resources.BadOmega;
				return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationError;
			}
			double num3 = 2.0 * Omega_s / (1.0 + 2.0 * Omega_s);
			if (Ps >= num3 * UpstrPress)
			{
				double num4 = Ps / UpstrPress;
				if (num4 > 1.0)
				{
					returnMsg = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BadEta_s");
					return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationError;
				}
				if (1.0 - 1.0 / num4 * ((2.0 * Omega_s - 1.0) / (2.0 * Omega_s)) <= 0.0)
				{
					Eta_c = num3;
				}
				else
				{
					Eta_c = num4 * (2.0 * Omega_s / (2.0 * Omega_s - 1.0)) * (1.0 - Math.Pow(1.0 - 1.0 / num4 * ((2.0 * Omega_s - 1.0) / (2.0 * Omega_s)), 0.5));
				}
				CriticPress = Eta_c * UpstrPress;
				double num5 = default(double);
				if (CriticPress >= TotBackPress)
				{
					flowType_isCritical = true;
					num5 = Eta_c;
				}
				else if (CriticPress < TotBackPress)
				{
					flowType_isCritical = false;
					num5 = TotBackPress / UpstrPress;
				}
				MassFlux = 68.09 * Math.Pow(2.0 * (1.0 - num4) + 2.0 * (Omega_s * num4 * Math.Log(num4 / num5) - (Omega_s - 1.0) * (num4 - num5)), 0.5) / (Omega_s * (num4 / num5 - 1.0) + 1.0) * Math.Pow(UpstrPress * num, 0.5);
			}
			else if (Ps < num3 * UpstrPress)
			{
				double num6 = default(double);
				if (Ps >= TotBackPress)
				{
					flowType_isCritical = true;
					num6 = Ps;
				}
				else if (Ps < TotBackPress)
				{
					flowType_isCritical = false;
					num6 = TotBackPress;
				}
				MassFlux = 96.3 * Math.Pow(num * (UpstrPress - num6), 0.5);
			}
			if (calculateKb)
			{
				Coeff_Kb = ValveUtilities.CalculateKb_figC2(ValveType, SetPress, AtmPress, TotBackPress, ref returnMsg, ref status);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(returnMsg))
				{
					status = CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationWarning;
				}
			}
			double num7 = RelievingFlow / num / 7.995;
			CalcOrifice = 0.3208 * num7 * num / (Coeff_Kd * Coeff_Kb * Coeff_Kc * MassFlux);
			return status;
		}

		public static CalculationStatusEnum CalculateOrificeSize_3(ref double CalcOrifice, ref double MassFlux, ref double CriticPress, ref double UpstrPress, ref double Omega, ref double Eta_calc, ref double Eta_v, ref double Eta_g, ref double Eta_vc, ref double Eta_gc, ref bool flowType_isCritical, double RelievingFlow, double RelievingTemp, ValveTypeEnum ValveType, ref double Coeff_Kb, double Coeff_Kc, double Coeff_Kd, bool calculateKb, double SetPress, double LowSetPressLimit, double OverPress, double AtmPress, double TotBackPress, double SpecHeatRatio, short opt_150_state, double xo, double V_vgo, double v9, double vo, double Y_go, double V_lo, double V_vo, double Alpha_o, double cp, double P_vo, double h_vlo, ref string returnMsg)
		{
			UpstrPress = (SetPress - AtmPress) * (1.0 + OverPress / 100.0) + AtmPress;
			Alpha_o = xo * V_vgo / vo;
			CalculationStatusEnum calcStatus = default(CalculationStatusEnum);
			switch (opt_150_state)
			{
			case 1:
			{
				if (Y_go <= 0.0 || Y_go > 1.0)
				{
					returnMsg = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BadY_go");
					return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationError;
				}
				double num2 = 1.0 / V_lo;
				double num3 = V_vo - V_lo;
				Omega = Alpha_o / SpecHeatRatio + 0.185 * (1.0 - Alpha_o) * num2 * cp * RelievingTemp * P_vo * Math.Pow(num3 / h_vlo, 2.0);
				if ((Omega < 0.01) | (Omega > 100.0))
				{
					returnMsg = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BadOmega");
					return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationError;
				}
				Eta_vc = Eta_c(Omega, ref returnMsg, ref calcStatus);
				double num4 = Alpha_o / SpecHeatRatio;
				if (num4 < 0.01 || num4 > 100.0)
				{
					returnMsg = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BadOmega");
					return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationError;
				}
				Eta_gc = Eta_c(num4, ref returnMsg, ref calcStatus);
				CriticPress = (Y_go * Eta_gc + (1.0 - Y_go) * Eta_vc) * UpstrPress;
				if (TotBackPress <= CriticPress)
				{
					flowType_isCritical = true;
					MassFlux = 68.09 * Math.Pow(UpstrPress / vo * (Y_go * Math.Pow(Eta_gc, 2.0) * SpecHeatRatio / Alpha_o + (1.0 - Y_go) * Math.Pow(Eta_vc, 2.0) / Omega), 0.5);
					break;
				}
				flowType_isCritical = false;
				double num = TotBackPress / UpstrPress;
				calcStatus = Eta_g_v(ref Eta_v, ref Eta_g, num, Y_go, Alpha_o, SpecHeatRatio, Omega, ref returnMsg);
				double x = 68.09 * Math.Pow(-2.0 * (Alpha_o / SpecHeatRatio * Math.Log(Eta_g) + (Alpha_o / SpecHeatRatio - 1.0) * (1.0 - Eta_g)), 0.5) / (Alpha_o / SpecHeatRatio * (1.0 / Eta_g - 1.0) + 1.0) * Math.Pow(UpstrPress / vo, 0.5);
				double x2 = 68.09 * Math.Pow(-2.0 * (Omega * Math.Log(Eta_v) + (Omega - 1.0) * (1.0 - Eta_v)), 0.5) / (Omega * (1.0 / Eta_v - 1.0) + 1.0) * Math.Pow(UpstrPress / vo, 0.5);
				MassFlux = Math.Pow(Y_go * Math.Pow(x, 2.0) + (1.0 - Y_go) * Math.Pow(x2, 2.0), 0.5);
				break;
			}
			case 2:
				Omega = 9.0 * (v9 / vo - 1.0);
				if ((Omega < 0.01) | (Omega > 100.0))
				{
					returnMsg = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BadOmega");
					return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationError;
				}
				Eta_calc = Eta_c(Omega, ref returnMsg, ref calcStatus);
				CriticPress = Eta_calc * UpstrPress;
				if (TotBackPress <= CriticPress)
				{
					flowType_isCritical = true;
					MassFlux = 68.09 * Eta_calc * Math.Pow(UpstrPress / (vo * Omega), 0.5);
				}
				else
				{
					flowType_isCritical = false;
					double num = TotBackPress / UpstrPress;
					MassFlux = 68.09 * Math.Pow(-2.0 * (Omega * Math.Log(num) + (Omega - 1.0) * (1.0 - num)), 0.5) / (Omega * (1.0 / num - 1.0) + 1.0) * Math.Pow(UpstrPress / vo, 0.5);
				}
				break;
			}
			if (calculateKb)
			{
				Coeff_Kb = ValveUtilities.CalculateKb(ValveType, SetPress, OverPress, AtmPress, TotBackPress, LowSetPressLimit, CriticPress, ref returnMsg);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(returnMsg))
				{
					calcStatus = CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationWarning;
				}
			}
			CalcOrifice = 0.04 * RelievingFlow / (Coeff_Kd * Coeff_Kb * Coeff_Kc * MassFlux);
			return calcStatus;
		}

		private static double Eta_c(double Omega, ref string returnMsg, ref CalculationStatusEnum calcStatus)
		{
			double num = 0.1;
			double num2 = 0.001;
			short num3 = 0;
			double num4 = ((!(Omega < 10.0)) ? 0.1 : 0.01);
			double num5;
			do
			{
				num5 = Math.Pow(num, 2.0) + (Math.Pow(Omega, 2.0) - 2.0 * Omega) * Math.Pow(1.0 - num, 2.0) + 2.0 * Math.Pow(Omega, 2.0) * Math.Log(num) + 2.0 * Math.Pow(Omega, 2.0) * (1.0 - num);
				num += num2;
				double num6 = Math.Pow(num, 2.0) + (Math.Pow(Omega, 2.0) - 2.0 * Omega) * Math.Pow(1.0 - num, 2.0) + 2.0 * Math.Pow(Omega, 2.0) * Math.Log(num) + 2.0 * Math.Pow(Omega, 2.0) * (1.0 - num);
				if (num5 * num6 < 0.0)
				{
					num -= num2;
					num2 /= 10.0;
				}
				else
				{
					num += num2;
				}
				num3 = checked((short)(num3 + 1));
				if (num3 > 100000)
				{
					returnMsg = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("TooManyEtaC");
					calcStatus = CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationWarning;
					num = (double)calcStatus;
					break;
				}
			}
			while (!(Math.Abs(num5) < num4));
			return num;
		}

		private static CalculationStatusEnum Eta_g_v(ref double Eta_v, ref double Eta_g, double Eta_a, double Y_go, double Alpha_o, double SpecHeatRatio, double Omega, ref string returnMsg)
		{
			Eta_v = 0.001;
			double num = 0.001;
			short num2 = 0;
			double num3 = ((!(Omega < 10.0)) ? 0.1 : 0.01);
			double num4;
			do
			{
				num4 = Alpha_o / SpecHeatRatio * (Y_go / (Eta_a - (1.0 - Y_go) * Eta_v) - 1.0) - Omega * (1.0 / Eta_v - 1.0);
				Eta_v += num;
				double num5 = Alpha_o / SpecHeatRatio * (Y_go / (Eta_a - (1.0 - Y_go) * Eta_v) - 1.0) - Omega * (1.0 / Eta_v - 1.0);
				if (num4 * num5 < 0.0)
				{
					Eta_v -= num;
					num /= 10.0;
				}
				else
				{
					Eta_v += num;
				}
				num2 = checked((short)(num2 + 1));
				if (num2 > 100000)
				{
					returnMsg = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("TooManyEtaC");
					return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationError;
				}
			}
			while (!(Math.Abs(num4) < num3));
			Eta_g = (Eta_a - (1.0 - Y_go) * Eta_v) / Y_go;
			return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationOK;
		}

		public static CalculationStatusEnum CalculateOrificeSize_4(ref double CalcOrifice, ref bool flowType_isCritical, ref double MassFlux_Design, List<DirectIntegrationStep> IntegrationResults, double RelievingFlow, double RelievingTemp, double RelievingVf, ValveTypeEnum ValveType, ref double Coeff_Kb, double Coeff_Kc, double Coeff_Kd, double Coeff_Kv, bool calculateKb, double SetPress, double OverPress, double TotBackPress, double UpstrPress, double InletLoss, IPPPFluid pppFluid, bool IsFireCalc, bool shouldReflash, ref string returnMsg)
		{
			double[] array = new double[201];
			double[] array2 = new double[201];
			double[] array3 = new double[201];
			double[] array4 = new double[201];
			double[] array5 = new double[201];
			_ = new double[201];
			_ = new double[201];
			double[] array6 = new double[201];
			array[0] = UpstrPress;
			if (!double.IsNaN(InletLoss))
			{
				array[0] -= InletLoss;
			}
			array2[0] = array[0] * 4633.062992125984;
			double num = RelievingTemp + -459.67;
			if (shouldReflash)
			{
				double num2 = UpstrPress * 6.89475908677537;
				bool flag;
				if (!IsFireCalc)
				{
					double t = 5.0 / 9.0 * (num + -32.0);
					flag = pppFluid.TPFlash(t, num2);
					if (!double.IsNaN(RelievingVf))
					{
						bool flag2 = false;
						if (RelievingVf == 0.0 || RelievingVf == 1.0)
						{
							if (RelievingVf != pppFluid.VaporFraction())
							{
								flag2 = true;
							}
						}
						else if (Math.Abs(RelievingVf - pppFluid.VaporFraction()) / RelievingVf > 0.0001)
						{
							flag2 = true;
						}
						if (flag2)
						{
							flag = pppFluid.PVFlash(num2, RelievingVf);
						}
					}
				}
				else
				{
					flag = pppFluid.PVFlashFire(num2, RelievingVf);
				}
				if (flag && !double.IsNaN(InletLoss) && InletLoss != 0.0)
				{
					flag = pppFluid.IsenthalpicFlash(num2 - InletLoss * 6.89475908677537);
				}
				if (!flag || double.IsNaN(pppFluid.Temperature()))
				{
					returnMsg = Resources.DirectFlashFailed;
					return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationError;
				}
			}
			short num3 = 0;
			bool flag3 = false;
			checked
			{
				while (!flag3)
				{
					if (num3 > 0)
					{
						array[num3] = 0.96 * array[num3 - 1];
						if (array[num3] < TotBackPress)
						{
							array[num3] = TotBackPress;
						}
						double num2 = array[num3] * 6.89475908677537;
						bool flag = (IsFireCalc ? pppFluid.IsentropicFlashFire(num2) : pppFluid.IsentropicFlash(num2));
						double t = pppFluid.Temperature();
						if (!flag || double.IsNaN(pppFluid.Temperature()))
						{
							returnMsg = Resources.DirectFlashFailed;
							return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationError;
						}
						num = 32.0 + 1.8 * t;
					}
					double vapQualMass = pppFluid.VaporMassFraction();
					array6[num3] = pppFluid.OverallDensity() * 0.062428;
					if (num3 > 0)
					{
						array2[num3] = array[num3] * 4633.062992125984;
						array3[num3] = (array2[num3] - array2[num3 - 1]) / (array6[num3] + array6[num3 - 1]) * 2.0;
						array4[num3] = array4[num3 - 1] + array3[num3];
						array5[num3] = Math.Sqrt(-2.0 * array4[num3]) * array6[num3];
					}
					else
					{
						array3[0] = 0.0;
						array4[0] = 0.0;
						array5[0] = 0.0;
					}
					IntegrationResults.Add(new DirectIntegrationStep
					{
						Pressure = array[num3],
						Temperature = num,
						VapQualMass = vapQualMass,
						Density = array6[num3],
						Integrand = array3[num3],
						Summation = array4[num3],
						MassFlux = array5[num3]
					});
					if (num3 > 0 && ((array[num3] <= TotBackPress) | (array5[num3] < array5[num3 - 1])))
					{
						flag3 = true;
						break;
					}
					num3++;
					if (num3 > 198)
					{
						returnMsg = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("ExceededNum");
						return CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationError;
					}
				}
				CalculationStatusEnum result = default(CalculationStatusEnum);
				if (calculateKb)
				{
					Coeff_Kb = ValveUtilities.CalculateKb(ValveType, SetPress, OverPress, 14.6959, TotBackPress, 50.0, array[num3 - 1], ref returnMsg);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(returnMsg))
					{
						result = CalculationStatusEnum.CalculationWarning;
					}
				}
				if (array[num3] > TotBackPress || array5[num3 - 1] > array5[num3])
				{
					flowType_isCritical = true;
					MassFlux_Design = array5[num3 - 1];
				}
				else
				{
					flowType_isCritical = false;
					MassFlux_Design = array5[num3];
				}
				CalcOrifice = 0.04 * RelievingFlow / (Coeff_Kd * Coeff_Kb * Coeff_Kc * Coeff_Kv * MassFlux_Design);
				return result;
			}
		}
	}
	public class OrificeSpecification
	{
		private string mDesignation;

		private double mArea;

		private bool mIsUser;

		public string Designation
		{
			get
			{
				return mDesignation;
			}
			set
			{
				mDesignation = value;
			}
		}

		public double Area
		{
			get
			{
				return mArea;
			}
			set
			{
				mArea = value;
			}
		}

		public bool IsUser
		{
			get
			{
				return mIsUser;
			}
			set
			{
				mIsUser = value;
			}
		}

		public double InletFlangeSize { get; set; }

		public double InletFlangeRating { get; set; }

		public double OutletFlangeSize { get; set; }

		public double OutletFlangeRating { get; set; }

		public OrificeSpecification(string designation, double area, bool user = false, double inletSize = double.NaN, double inletRating = double.NaN, double outletSize = double.NaN, double outletRating = double.NaN)
		{
			mDesignation = designation;
			mArea = area;
			mIsUser = user;
			if (user)
			{
				InletFlangeSize = inletSize;
				InletFlangeRating = inletRating;
				OutletFlangeSize = outletSize;
				OutletFlangeRating = outletRating;
			}
			else
			{
				InletFlangeSize = double.NaN;
				InletFlangeRating = double.NaN;
				OutletFlangeSize = double.NaN;
				OutletFlangeRating = double.NaN;
			}
		}
	}
	public class OrificeSelection
	{
		private string UOMCalcOrifice;

		private string mValveDesignation;

		private string mValveInRating;

		private string mValveInSize;

		private string mValveOutSize;

		private string mValveOutRating;

		private List<OrificeSpecification> mOrificeSpecArray;

		private string mErrorMessage;

		public string ValveDesignation => mValveDesignation;

		public string ValveInRating => mValveInRating;

		public string ValveInSize => mValveInSize;

		public string ValveOutSize => mValveOutSize;

		public string ValveOutRating => mValveOutRating;

		public IList<OrificeSpecification> AvailableOrifices
		{
			get
			{
				return mOrificeSpecArray;
			}
			set
			{
				mOrificeSpecArray = (List<OrificeSpecification>)value;
			}
		}

		public string ErrorMessage => mErrorMessage;

		public OrificeSelection(string UOMCalcOrifice)
		{
			OrificeListInitialize(UOMCalcOrifice);
		}

		private void OrificeListInitialize(string UOMCalcOrifice)
		{
			mOrificeSpecArray = new List<OrificeSpecification>();
			IList<OrificeSpecification> list = APIStandardOrifice(UOMCalcOrifice);
			foreach (OrificeSpecification item in list)
			{
				mOrificeSpecArray.Add(item);
			}
		}

		public bool OrificeDesignate(double SetPress, double DesTemp, string SelectOrifice, double TotBackPress, string ValveType, string ValveMaterial)
		{
			bool result = true;
			if ((Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Pilot Operated", TextCompare: false) == 0) & (Operators.CompareString(ValveMaterial, "Chrome / Molybd.", TextCompare: false) == 0))
			{
				mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("CMBody");
				return false;
			}
			checked
			{
				short num = (short)(mOrificeSpecArray.Count - 1);
				short num3 = default(short);
				for (short num2 = 0; num2 <= num; num2 = (short)unchecked(num2 + 1))
				{
					if (mOrificeSpecArray[num2].Area == Conversions.ToDouble(SelectOrifice))
					{
						num3 = num2;
					}
				}
				OrificeSpecification orificeSpecification = mOrificeSpecArray[num3];
				if (num3 <= 2 || num3 >= 17 || orificeSpecification.IsUser)
				{
					mValveDesignation = mOrificeSpecArray[num3].Designation;
					mValveInSize = Conversions.ToString(double.IsNaN(orificeSpecification.InletFlangeSize) ? "" : ((object)orificeSpecification.InletFlangeSize));
					mValveInRating = Conversions.ToString(double.IsNaN(orificeSpecification.InletFlangeRating) ? "" : ((object)orificeSpecification.InletFlangeRating));
					mValveOutSize = Conversions.ToString(double.IsNaN(orificeSpecification.OutletFlangeSize) ? "" : ((object)orificeSpecification.OutletFlangeSize));
					mValveOutRating = Conversions.ToString(double.IsNaN(orificeSpecification.OutletFlangeRating) ? "" : ((object)orificeSpecification.OutletFlangeRating));
					return true;
				}
				switch (num3)
				{
				case 3:
					mValveDesignation = orificeSpecification.Designation;
					result = Orifice_D(SetPress, DesTemp, TotBackPress, ValveType, ValveMaterial);
					break;
				case 4:
					mValveDesignation = orificeSpecification.Designation;
					result = Orifice_E(SetPress, DesTemp, TotBackPress, ValveType, ValveMaterial);
					break;
				case 5:
					mValveDesignation = orificeSpecification.Designation;
					result = Orifice_F(SetPress, DesTemp, TotBackPress, ValveType, ValveMaterial);
					break;
				case 6:
					mValveDesignation = orificeSpecification.Designation;
					result = Orifice_G(SetPress, DesTemp, TotBackPress, ValveType, ValveMaterial);
					break;
				case 7:
					mValveDesignation = orificeSpecification.Designation;
					result = Orifice_H(SetPress, DesTemp, TotBackPress, ValveType, ValveMaterial);
					break;
				case 8:
					mValveDesignation = orificeSpecification.Designation;
					result = Orifice_J(SetPress, DesTemp, TotBackPress, ValveType, ValveMaterial);
					break;
				case 9:
					mValveDesignation = orificeSpecification.Designation;
					result = Orifice_K(SetPress, DesTemp, TotBackPress, ValveType, ValveMaterial);
					break;
				case 10:
					mValveDesignation = orificeSpecification.Designation;
					result = Orifice_L(SetPress, DesTemp, TotBackPress, ValveType, ValveMaterial);
					break;
				case 11:
					mValveDesignation = orificeSpecification.Designation;
					result = Orifice_M(SetPress, DesTemp, TotBackPress, ValveType, ValveMaterial);
					break;
				case 12:
					mValveDesignation = orificeSpecification.Designation;
					result = Orifice_N(SetPress, DesTemp, TotBackPress, ValveType, ValveMaterial);
					break;
				case 13:
					mValveDesignation = orificeSpecification.Designation;
					result = Orifice_P(SetPress, DesTemp, TotBackPress, ValveType, ValveMaterial);
					break;
				case 14:
					mValveDesignation = orificeSpecification.Designation;
					result = Orifice_Q(SetPress, DesTemp, TotBackPress, ValveType, ValveMaterial);
					break;
				case 15:
					mValveDesignation = orificeSpecification.Designation;
					result = Orifice_R(SetPress, DesTemp, TotBackPress, ValveType, ValveMaterial);
					break;
				case 16:
					mValveDesignation = orificeSpecification.Designation;
					result = Orifice_T(SetPress, DesTemp, TotBackPress, ValveType, ValveMaterial);
					break;
				}
				orificeSpecification = null;
				return result;
			}
		}

		private bool Orifice_E(double SetPress, double DesTemp, double TotBackPress, string ValveType, string ValveMaterial)
		{
			if ((Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Conventional", TextCompare: false) == 0) | (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Balanced-Bellow", TextCompare: false) == 0))
			{
				switch (ValveMaterial)
				{
				case "Carbon Steel":
					break;
				case "Chrome / Molybd.":
					goto IL_0b14;
				case "Stainless Steel":
					goto IL_113f;
				case "Nickel / Copper Alloy":
					goto IL_1faa;
				case "Alloy 20":
					goto IL_21b5;
				default:
					goto IL_3683;
				}
				if (DesTemp >= -20.0 && DesTemp <= 100.0)
				{
					if (SetPress < 285.0)
					{
						if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Conventional", TextCompare: false) == 0)
						{
							if (TotBackPress <= 285.0)
							{
								goto IL_369d;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
						else
						{
							if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Balanced-Bellow", TextCompare: false) != 0)
							{
								goto IL_3683;
							}
							if (TotBackPress <= 230.0)
							{
								goto IL_369d;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
					}
					else if (SetPress >= 285.0 && SetPress < 740.0)
					{
						if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Conventional", TextCompare: false) == 0)
						{
							if (TotBackPress <= 285.0)
							{
								goto IL_36d0;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
						else
						{
							if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Balanced-Bellow", TextCompare: false) != 0)
							{
								goto IL_3683;
							}
							if (TotBackPress <= 230.0)
							{
								goto IL_36d0;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
					}
					else if (SetPress >= 740.0 && SetPress < 1480.0)
					{
						if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Conventional", TextCompare: false) == 0)
						{
							if (TotBackPress <= 285.0)
							{
								goto IL_3703;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
						else
						{
							if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Balanced-Bellow", TextCompare: false) != 0)
							{
								goto IL_3683;
							}
							if (TotBackPress <= 230.0)
							{
								goto IL_3703;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
					}
					else if (SetPress >= 1480.0 && SetPress < 2220.0)
					{
						if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Conventional", TextCompare: false) == 0)
						{
							if (TotBackPress <= 600.0)
							{
								goto IL_3736;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
						else
						{
							if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Balanced-Bellow", TextCompare: false) != 0)
							{
								goto IL_3683;
							}
							if (TotBackPress <= 500.0)
							{

		private bool Orifice_F(double SetPress, double DesTemp, double TotBackPress, string ValveType, string ValveMaterial)
		{
			if ((Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Conventional", TextCompare: false) == 0) | (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Balanced-Bellow", TextCompare: false) == 0))
			{
				switch (ValveMaterial)
				{
				case "Carbon Steel":
					break;
				case "Chrome / Molybd.":
					goto IL_0b14;
				case "Stainless Steel":
					goto IL_113f;
				case "Nickel / Copper Alloy":
					goto IL_1faa;
				case "Alloy 20":
					goto IL_21b5;
				default:
					goto IL_3683;
				}
				if (DesTemp >= -20.0 && DesTemp <= 100.0)
				{
					if (SetPress < 285.0)
					{
						if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Conventional", TextCompare: false) == 0)
						{
							if (TotBackPress <= 285.0)
							{
								goto IL_369d;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
						else
						{
							if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Balanced-Bellow", TextCompare: false) != 0)
							{
								goto IL_3683;
							}
							if (TotBackPress <= 230.0)
							{
								goto IL_369d;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
					}
					else if (SetPress >= 285.0 && SetPress < 740.0)
					{
						if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Conventional", TextCompare: false) == 0)
						{
							if (TotBackPress <= 285.0)
							{
								goto IL_36d0;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
						else
						{
							if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Balanced-Bellow", TextCompare: false) != 0)
							{

							}

							if (TotBackPress <= 285.0)

		private bool Orifice_G(double SetPress, double DesTemp, double TotBackPress, string ValveType, string ValveMaterial)
		{
			if ((Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Conventional", TextCompare: false) == 0) | (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Balanced-Bellow", TextCompare: false) == 0))
			{
				switch (ValveMaterial)
				{
				case "Carbon Steel":
					break;
				case "Chrome / Molybd.":
					goto IL_0b14;
				case "Stainless Steel":
					goto IL_113f;
				case "Nickel / Copper Alloy":
					goto IL_1faa;
				case "Alloy 20":
					goto IL_21b5;
				default:
					goto IL_3683;
				}
				if (DesTemp >= -20.0 && DesTemp <= 100.0)
				{
					if (SetPress < 285.0)
					{
						if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Conventional", TextCompare: false) == 0)
						{
							if (TotBackPress <= 285.0)
							{
								goto IL_369d;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
						else
						{
							if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Balanced-Bellow", TextCompare: false) != 0)
							{
								goto IL_3683;
							}
							if (TotBackPress <= 230.0)
							{
								goto IL_369d;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
					}
					else if (SetPress >= 285.0 && SetPress < 740.0)
					{
						if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Conventional", TextCompare: false) == 0)
						{
							if (TotBackPress <= 285.0)
							{

							}

						}
						else
						{

							{

							}

							if (TotBackPress <= 285.0)

					if (TotBackPress <= 720.0)
					{
						goto IL_3802;
					}
					mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
				}
				else if (SetPress > 1080.0 && SetPress <= 1800.0)
				{

						goto IL_3868;
					}

			mValveOutRating = "150";

			mValveInRating = "1500";
			mValveOutSize = "3";
			mValveOutRating = "300";
			return true;

		private bool Orifice_J(double SetPress, double DesTemp, double TotBackPress, string ValveType, string ValveMaterial)
		{
			if ((Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Conventional", TextCompare: false) == 0) | (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Balanced-Bellow", TextCompare: false) == 0))
			{
				switch (ValveMaterial)
				{
				case "Carbon Steel":
					break;
				case "Chrome / Molybd.":
					goto IL_0961;
				case "Stainless Steel":
					goto IL_0e6a;
				case "Nickel / Copper Alloy":
					goto IL_1a91;
				case "Alloy 20":
					goto IL_1c9c;
				default:
					goto IL_2e57;
				}
				if (DesTemp >= -20.0 && DesTemp <= 100.0)
				{
					if (SetPress < 285.0)
					{
						if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Conventional", TextCompare: false) == 0)
						{
							if (TotBackPress <= 285.0)
							{
								goto IL_2e71;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
						else
						{
							if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Balanced-Bellow", TextCompare: false) != 0)
							{
								goto IL_2e57;
							}
							if (TotBackPress <= 230.0)
							{
								goto IL_2e71;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
					}
					else if (SetPress >= 285.0 && SetPress < 740.0)
					{
						if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Conventional", TextCompare: false) == 0)
						{
							if (TotBackPress <= 285.0)
							{
								goto IL_2ea4;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
						else
						{
							if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Balanced-Bellow", TextCompare: false) != 0)
							{
								goto IL_2e57;
							}
							if (TotBackPress <= 230.0)
							{
								goto IL_2ea4;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
					}
					else if (SetPress >= 740.0 && SetPress < 1480.0)
					{
						if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Conventional", TextCompare: false) == 0)
						{
							if (TotBackPress <= 285.0)
							{
								goto IL_2ed7;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
						else
						{
							if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Balanced-Bellow", TextCompare: false) != 0)
							{
								goto IL_2e57;
							}
							if (TotBackPress <= 230.0)
							{
								goto IL_2ed7;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
					}
					else if (SetPress >= 1480.0 && SetPress < 2220.0)
					{
						if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Conventional", TextCompare: false) == 0)
						{
							if (TotBackPress <= 285.0)
							{
								goto IL_2f0a;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
						else
						{
							if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Balanced-Bellow", TextCompare: false) != 0)
							{
								goto IL_2e57;
							}
							if (TotBackPress <= 230.0)
							{
								goto IL_2f0a;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
					}
					else if (SetPress >= 2220.0 && SetPress < 2700.0)
					{
						if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Conventional", TextCompare: false) == 0)
						{
							if (TotBackPress <= 600.0)
							{
								goto IL_2f3a;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
						else
						{
							if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Balanced-Bellow", TextCompare: false) != 0)
							{
								goto IL_2e57;
							}
							if (TotBackPress <= 230.0)
							{
								goto IL_2f3a;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
					}
					else
					{
						mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("SetPTooHigh");
					}
				}
				else if (DesTemp > 100.0 && DesTemp <= 450.0)
				{
					if (SetPress <= 185.0)
					{
						if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Conventional", TextCompare: false) == 0)
						{
							if (TotBackPress <= 285.0)
							{
								goto IL_2e71;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
						else
						{
							if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Balanced-Bellow", TextCompare: false) != 0)
							{
								goto IL_2e57;
							}
							if (TotBackPress <= 230.0)
							{

					mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
				}
				else

			}

					{
						goto IL_2e71;
					}
					mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");

					}
					mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
				}
				else if (SetPress > 930.0 && SetPress <= 1395.0)
				{
					if (TotBackPress <= 720.0)
					{
						goto IL_2f0a;
					}
					mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
				}

					}

				}
				else
				{
					mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("SetPTooHigh");
				}
			}
			else
			{
				mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BadDesT");
			}
			goto IL_2f6a;

			mValveInSize = "";
			mValveInRating = "";
			mValveOutSize = "";
			mValveOutRating = "";
			return false;
			IL_2967:
			if (DesTemp >= -20.0 && DesTemp <= 500.0)
			{
				if (SetPress <= 140.0)
				{
					if (TotBackPress <= 275.0)

						goto IL_2e71;

					}
					mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
				}

				{

					}

		private bool Orifice_L(double SetPress, double DesTemp, double TotBackPress, string ValveType, string ValveMaterial)
		{
			if ((Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Conventional", TextCompare: false) == 0) | (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Balanced-Bellow", TextCompare: false) == 0))
			{
				switch (ValveMaterial)
				{
				case "Carbon Steel":
					break;
				case "Chrome / Molybd.":
					goto IL_083f;
				case "Stainless Steel":
					goto IL_0cb7;
				case "Nickel / Copper Alloy":
					goto IL_169a;
				case "Alloy 20":
					goto IL_18a5;
				default:
					goto IL_2a60;
				}
				if (DesTemp >= -20.0 && DesTemp <= 100.0)
				{
					if (SetPress < 285.0)
					{
						if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Conventional", TextCompare: false) == 0)
						{
							if (TotBackPress <= 285.0)
							{
								goto IL_2a7a;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
						else
						{
							if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Balanced-Bellow", TextCompare: false) != 0)
							{
								goto IL_2a60;
							}
							if (TotBackPress <= 100.0)
							{
								goto IL_2a7a;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
					}
					else if (SetPress >= 285.0 && SetPress < 740.0)
					{
						if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Conventional", TextCompare: false) == 0)
						{
							if (TotBackPress <= 285.0)
							{
								goto IL_2aad;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
						else
						{
							if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Balanced-Bellow", TextCompare: false) != 0)
							{
								goto IL_2a60;
							}
							if (TotBackPress <= 170.0)
							{
								goto IL_2aad;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
					}
					else if (SetPress >= 740.0 && SetPress < 1000.0)
					{
						if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Conventional", TextCompare: false) == 0)
						{
							if (TotBackPress <= 285.0)
							{
								goto IL_2ae0;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
						else
						{
							if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Balanced-Bellow", TextCompare: false) != 0)
							{
								goto IL_2a60;
							}
							if (TotBackPress <= 170.0)
							{

							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
					}
					else if (SetPress >= 1000.0 && SetPress < 1500.0)
					{
						if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Conventional", TextCompare: false) == 0)
						{
							if (TotBackPress <= 285.0)
							{

							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
						else
						{
							if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Balanced-Bellow", TextCompare: false) != 0)

				else if (DesTemp > 100.0 && DesTemp <= 450.0)
				{
					if (SetPress <= 185.0)

		private bool Orifice_M(double SetPress, double DesTemp, double TotBackPress, string ValveType, string ValveMaterial)
		{
			if ((Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Conventional", TextCompare: false) == 0) | (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Balanced-Bellow", TextCompare: false) == 0))
			{
				switch (ValveMaterial)
				{
				case "Carbon Steel":
					break;
				case "Chrome / Molybd.":
					goto IL_068c;
				case "Stainless Steel":
					goto IL_09e2;
				case "Nickel / Copper Alloy":
					goto IL_1181;
				case "Alloy 20":
					goto IL_138c;
				default:
					goto IL_2394;
				}
				if (DesTemp >= -20.0 && DesTemp <= 100.0)
				{
					if (SetPress < 285.0)
					{
						if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Conventional", TextCompare: false) == 0)
						{
							if (TotBackPress <= 285.0)
							{
								goto IL_23ae;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
						else
						{
							if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Balanced-Bellow", TextCompare: false) != 0)
							{
								goto IL_2394;
							}
							if (TotBackPress <= 80.0)
							{
								goto IL_23ae;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
					}
					else if (SetPress >= 285.0 && SetPress < 740.0)
					{
						if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Conventional", TextCompare: false) == 0)
						{
							if (TotBackPress <= 285.0)
							{
								goto IL_23e1;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
						else
						{
							if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Balanced-Bellow", TextCompare: false) != 0)
							{
								goto IL_2394;
							}
							if (TotBackPress <= 160.0)
							{
								goto IL_23e1;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
					}
					else if (SetPress >= 740.0 && SetPress < 1100.0)
					{
						if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Conventional", TextCompare: false) == 0)
						{
							if (TotBackPress <= 285.0)
							{
								goto IL_2414;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
						else
						{
							if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Balanced-Bellow", TextCompare: false) != 0)
							{
								goto IL_2394;
							}
							if (TotBackPress <= 160.0)
							{
								goto IL_2414;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
					}
					else
					{
						mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("SetPTooHigh");
					}
				}
				else if (DesTemp > 100.0 && DesTemp <= 450.0)
				{
					if (SetPress <= 185.0)
					{
						if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Conventional", TextCompare: false) == 0)
						{
							if (TotBackPress <= 285.0)
							{
								goto IL_23ae;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
						else
						{
							if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Balanced-Bellow", TextCompare: false) != 0)
							{
								goto IL_2394;
							}
							if (TotBackPress <= 80.0)
							{
								goto IL_23ae;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
					}
					else if (SetPress > 185.0 && SetPress <= 615.0)
					{
						if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Conventional", TextCompare: false) == 0)
						{
							if (TotBackPress <= 285.0)
							{
								goto IL_23e1;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
						else
						{
							if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Balanced-Bellow", TextCompare: false) != 0)
							{
								goto IL_2394;
							}
							if (TotBackPress <= 160.0)
							{
								goto IL_23e1;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
					}
					else if (SetPress > 615.0 && SetPress <= 1100.0)
					{
						if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Conventional", TextCompare: false) == 0)
						{
							if (TotBackPress <= 285.0)

							{

						{

							{
								goto IL_23e1;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}

			IL_205f:

		public bool Orifice_N(double SetPress, double DesTemp, double TotBackPress, string ValveType, string ValveMaterial)
		{
			if ((Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Conventional", TextCompare: false) == 0) | (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Balanced-Bellow", TextCompare: false) == 0))
			{
				switch (ValveMaterial)
				{
				case "Carbon Steel":
					break;
				case "Chrome / Molybd.":
					goto IL_068c;
				case "Stainless Steel":
					goto IL_09e2;
				case "Nickel / Copper Alloy":
					goto IL_1181;
				case "Alloy 20":
					goto IL_138c;
				default:
					goto IL_2394;
				}
				if (DesTemp >= -20.0 && DesTemp <= 100.0)
				{
					if (SetPress < 285.0)
					{
						if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Conventional", TextCompare: false) == 0)
						{
							if (TotBackPress <= 285.0)
							{
								goto IL_23ae;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
						else
						{
							if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Balanced-Bellow", TextCompare: false) != 0)
							{
								goto IL_2394;
							}
							if (TotBackPress <= 80.0)
							{
								goto IL_23ae;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
					}
					else if (SetPress >= 285.0 && SetPress < 740.0)
					{
						if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Conventional", TextCompare: false) == 0)
						{
							if (TotBackPress <= 285.0)
							{
								goto IL_23e1;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
						else
						{
							if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Balanced-Bellow", TextCompare: false) != 0)
							{
								goto IL_2394;
							}
							if (TotBackPress <= 160.0)
							{
								goto IL_23e1;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
					}
					else if (SetPress >= 740.0 && SetPress < 1000.0)
					{
						if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Conventional", TextCompare: false) == 0)
						{
							if (TotBackPress <= 285.0)
							{
								goto IL_2414;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
						else
						{
							if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Balanced-Bellow", TextCompare: false) != 0)
							{
								goto IL_2394;
							}
							if (TotBackPress <= 160.0)
							{
								goto IL_2414;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
					}
					else
					{
						mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("SetPTooHigh");
					}
				}
				else if (DesTemp > 100.0 && DesTemp <= 450.0)
				{
					if (SetPress <= 185.0)
					{
						if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Conventional", TextCompare: false) == 0)
						{
							if (TotBackPress <= 285.0)

							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}

							}

							{

							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
						else

							{

							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");

					}

						}
						else
						{

							{

					}

				else

				}
			}
			else

				if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Pilot Operated", TextCompare: false) != 0)
				{

				}
				switch (ValveMaterial)
				{
				case "Carbon Steel":
					break;
				case "Stainless Steel":
					goto IL_1b5d;
				case "Nickel / Copper Alloy":
					goto IL_1ea4;
				case "Alloy 20":
					goto IL_205f;
				default:
					goto IL_2394;
				}
				if (DesTemp >= -20.0 && DesTemp <= 100.0)
				{
					if (SetPress < 285.0)
					{
						if (TotBackPress <= 285.0)
						{
							goto IL_23ae;
						}
						mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
					}
					else if (SetPress >= 285.0 && SetPress < 740.0)
					{
						if (TotBackPress <= 285.0)

					}

					{

						{
							goto IL_23e1;
						}
						mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
					}
					else if (SetPress > 600.0 && SetPress <= 1200.0)
					{
						if (TotBackPress <= 285.0)
						{
							goto IL_2414;
						}
						mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
					}
					else if (SetPress > 1200.0 && SetPress <= 1795.0)
					{
						if (TotBackPress <= 740.0)
						{
							goto IL_247a;
						}

					mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");

			mValveInRating = "900";
			mValveOutSize = "6";

		private bool Orifice_P(double SetPress, double DesTemp, double TotBackPress, string ValveType, string ValveMaterial)
		{
			if ((Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Conventional", TextCompare: false) == 0) | (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Balanced-Bellow", TextCompare: false) == 0))
			{
				switch (ValveMaterial)
				{
				case "Carbon Steel":
					break;
				case "Chrome / Molybd.":
					goto IL_068c;
				case "Stainless Steel":
					goto IL_09e2;
				case "Nickel / Copper Alloy":
					goto IL_1181;
				case "Alloy 20":
					goto IL_138c;
				default:
					goto IL_2461;
				}
				if (DesTemp >= -20.0 && DesTemp <= 100.0)
				{
					if (SetPress < 285.0)
					{
						if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Conventional", TextCompare: false) == 0)

							if (TotBackPress <= 285.0)
							{
								goto IL_247b;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
						else
						{
							if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Balanced-Bellow", TextCompare: false) != 0)
							{
								goto IL_2461;
							}
							if (TotBackPress <= 80.0)
							{
								goto IL_247b;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
					}
					else if (SetPress >= 285.0 && SetPress < 525.0)

						{
							if (TotBackPress <= 285.0)
							{

						}

						{

							{
								goto IL_2461;
							}
							if (TotBackPress <= 150.0)
							{
								goto IL_24ae;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
					}
					else if (SetPress >= 525.0 && SetPress < 1000.0)

						if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Conventional", TextCompare: false) == 0)
						{
							if (TotBackPress <= 285.0)
							{

						}

				case "Alloy 20":

				default:

				}

			IL_15e3:

		private bool Orifice_R(double SetPress, double DesTemp, double TotBackPress, string ValveType, string ValveMaterial)
		{
			if ((Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Conventional", TextCompare: false) == 0) | (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Balanced-Bellow", TextCompare: false) == 0))
			{
				switch (ValveMaterial)
				{
				case "Carbon Steel":
					break;
				case "Chrome / Molybd.":
					goto IL_0648;
				case "Stainless Steel":
					goto IL_07c2;
				case "Nickel / Copper Alloy":
					goto IL_0e83;
				case "Alloy 20":
					goto IL_108e;
				default:
					goto IL_1a4a;
				}
				if (DesTemp >= -20.0 && DesTemp <= 100.0)
				{
					if (SetPress < 100.0)
					{

						{
							if (TotBackPress <= 60.0)
							{
								goto IL_1a64;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
						else
						{
							if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Balanced-Bellow", TextCompare: false) != 0)
							{
								goto IL_1a4a;
							}
							if (TotBackPress <= 60.0)
							{
								goto IL_1a64;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
					}
					else if (SetPress >= 100.0 && SetPress < 230.0)
					{

							if (TotBackPress <= 100.0)
							{

							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
						else

							{
								goto IL_1a4a;
							}
							if (TotBackPress <= 100.0)
							{
								goto IL_1aca;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
					}
					else if (SetPress >= 230.0 && SetPress <= 300.0)
					{
						if (Operators.CompareString(ValveType, "Conventional", TextCompare: false) == 0)
						{
							if (TotBackPress <= 100.0)
							{
								goto IL_1b2d;
							}
							mErrorMessage = Resources.ResourceManager.GetString("BPHigh");
						}
						else
